# List 19

## Unit 1

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>ponderable</b></del></td><td>/ˈpɑːndərəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有价值的，值得考虑的</td><td>a ponderable question<br>值得考虑的问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Whether growth always brings happiness is a genuinely ponderable question.  增长是否总带来幸福，是个真正值得考虑的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appreciable</td><td>/əˈpriːʃəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可感知的，明显的</td><td>an appreciable difference<br>明显的差别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There is no appreciable difference between these two brands of paint.  这两个牌子的油漆之间没有明显的差别。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perceptible</td><td>/pərˈseptəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可察觉的</td><td>a barely perceptible change<br>几乎察觉不到的变化</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a barely perceptible change in her tone of voice.  她的语调有一丝几乎察觉不到的变化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensible</td><td>/ˈsensəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>明智的，合理的</td><td>a sensible choice<br>明智的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wearing boots was a sensible choice in that mud.  在那种泥地里穿靴子是明智的选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inappreciable</del></td><td>/ˌɪnəˈpriʃəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，感觉不到的</td><td>an inappreciable amount<br>微不足道的量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The risk from such a tiny dose is inappreciable.  如此微小剂量带来的风险微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ponderous</b></td><td>/ˈpɑːndərəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的</td><td>ponderous machinery<br>笨重的机器</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ponderous machinery filled the workshop and shook the floor all day.  笨重的机器占满车间，整天震动地板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cumbersome</td><td>/ˈkʌmbərsəm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>笨重的，难处理的</td><td>a cumbersome procedure<br>繁琐的手续</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old permit system was so cumbersome that few farmers bothered applying.  旧的许可制度太繁琐，很少有农民愿意申请。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heavy</td><td>/ˈhevi/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>重的，沉重的</td><td>too heavy to lift<br>重得抬不动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The box was so heavy that two men could barely move it.  这箱子太重，两个人几乎搬不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hefty</td><td>/ˈhefti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉重的；大量的</td><td>a hefty volume<br>一本厚重的书</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He carried a hefty volume of legal cases under his arm.  他腋下夹着一本厚重的法律案例集。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 massive</td><td>/ˈmæsɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，厚重的</td><td>a massive iron gate<br>厚重的铁门</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A massive wave struck the harbor wall just after midnight.  午夜刚过，一个巨浪撞上了防波堤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 onerous</td><td>/ˈɑːnərəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁重的，费力的</td><td>onerous responsibilities<br>繁重的责任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Caring for the archive proved more onerous than the volunteers had expected.  照管这份档案比志愿者们预想的更费时费心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weighty</td><td>/ˈweɪti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>重要的，重大的；沉重的</td><td>weighty matters<br>重大的事务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The elders debated weighty questions of land and water.  长者们讨论着土地与水源的重大问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ethereal</td><td>/iˈθɪriəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻巧精致的；非物质的，精神（上）的</td><td>an ethereal beauty<br>轻灵飘逸的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her voice had an ethereal quality that hushed the entire hall.  她的嗓音有种轻灵飘逸的质感，让全场安静下来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 gossamer</td><td>/ˈɡɑːsəmər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>虚无缥缈的东西；轻薄的；薄弱的</td><td>gossamer wings<br>薄如蝉翼的翅膀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her gossamer scarf floated on the breeze like a wisp of smoke.  她轻薄如蝉翼的围巾在微风中飘动，像一缕青烟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 light</td><td>/ˈlaɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>轻微的，不严重的</td><td>a light meal<br>一顿便餐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He suffered only light injuries in the accident.  他在事故中只受了轻伤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 weightless</td><td>/ˈweɪtləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无重量的，失重的</td><td>feel almost weightless<br>感觉几乎失重</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Astronauts float weightless while the station circles the earth.  空间站绕地飞行时，航天员在失重中漂浮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pontificate</b></td><td>/pɑːnˈtɪfɪkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>傲慢地做或说</td><td>pontificate about politics<br>傲慢地大谈政治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He loves to pontificate about politics to anyone who will listen.  他喜欢对任何愿意听的人傲慢地大谈政治。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 condescend</td><td>/ˌkɑːndɪˈsend/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>居高临下地对待；俯就</td><td>condescend to his students<br>对学生摆架子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The lecturer condescended to his students in every class.  这位讲师每节课都对学生摆架子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pore</b></td><td>/pɔːr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>钻研；仔细阅读；凝视</td><td>pore over old letters<br>仔细研读旧信件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She pored over the old letters for hours without saying anything.  她埋头细读那些旧信数小时，一言不发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 cogitate</del></td><td>/ˈkɑːdʒɪˌteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>认真思考，仔细考虑</td><td>cogitate on the problem<br>仔细思考这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and cogitated on his next move.  他坐在窗边，仔细思量下一步该怎么走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 consider</td><td>/kənˈsɪdər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，斟酌</td><td>consider the options carefully<br>仔细斟酌各种选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She considered the options carefully before signing the lease.  她在签租约前仔细斟酌了各种选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 contemplate</td><td>/ˈkɑːntəmpleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思，仔细思索</td><td>contemplate the consequences<br>仔细思索后果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She sat by the window and contemplated the few choices left to her.  她坐在窗边，仔细思索留给自己的几个选择。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 meditate</td><td>/ˈmedɪteɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>思索，沉思</td><td>meditate on the problem<br>沉思这个问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and meditated on the day's events.  他坐在窗边沉思白天发生的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 perpend</del></td><td>/pərˈpɛnd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>仔细考虑，斟酌</td><td>perpend the evidence<br>仔细斟酌证据</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge asked the jury to perpend the evidence overnight.  法官要陪审团用一夜时间仔细斟酌证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruminate</td><td>/ˈruːmɪneɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>沉思；反复琢磨；反刍</td><td>ruminate on the past<br>反复思量往事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sat by the window and ruminated on the past.  他坐在窗边，反复回想往事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 study</td><td>/ˈstʌdi/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>学习，研究</td><td>study hard for exams<br>为考试用功</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She studies medieval history at a university in Scotland.  她在苏格兰一所大学研究中世纪史。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 weigh</td><td>/ˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>权衡，仔细考虑</td><td>weigh the risks<br>权衡各种风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They weighed the risks for several hours before agreeing.  他们权衡风险数小时后才达成一致。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chew over</td><td>/tʃu ˈoʊvər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>反复琢磨，仔细考虑</td><td>chew over the offer<br>反复琢磨那份提议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Give me a night to chew over your generous offer.  给我一晚时间好好琢磨你这份大方的提议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flip</td><td>/ˈflɪp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>轻率的，不恭的；轻抛</td><td>a flip answer<br>轻率的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flip reply annoyed the interviewer.  他轻率的回答让面试官不快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 glance</td><td>/ˈɡlæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>扫视，一瞥</td><td>glance at his watch<br>看一眼手表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She glanced at her watch and hurried toward the front door.  她看了一眼手表，匆匆走向前门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 leaf</td><td>/ˈliːf/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>翻阅，快速翻看</td><td>leaf through a magazine<br>翻阅一本杂志</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He leafed through the old magazine while waiting for coffee.  他等咖啡时翻阅那本旧杂志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 riffle</td><td>/ˈrɪfəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>快速翻动，翻阅</td><td>riffle through the files<br>快速翻查档案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She riffled through the files looking for one missing page.  她快速翻查档案，寻找那缺失的一页。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 skim</td><td>/ˈskɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>略读，浏览</td><td>skim the report quickly<br>快速浏览报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She skimmed the long report during the short taxi ride.  她在短短的出租车路程中浏览了那份长报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>porous</b></td><td>/ˈpɔːrəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>多孔可渗透的</td><td>porous volcanic rock<br>多孔的火山岩</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water seeps very quickly through the porous volcanic rock here.  水很快渗过这里多孔的火山岩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 passable</td><td>/ˈpæsəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可通行的；尚可的</td><td>a passable mountain road<br>可通行的山路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The road is barely passable after the spring floods.  春季洪水过后，这条路勉强可以通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penetrable</td><td>/ˈpenɪtrəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可穿透的，可渗透的</td><td>easily penetrable armor<br>易被穿透的护甲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thin plywood is penetrable by an ordinary nail.  普通钉子就能穿透薄胶合板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 permeable</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmiəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可渗透的</td><td>a permeable rock layer<br>可渗水的岩层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water collects here because the rock layer below is highly permeable.  水在这里积聚，因为下面的岩层极易渗水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pervious</td><td>/ˈpɜːrviəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可渗透的</td><td>pervious concrete surface<br>透水的混凝土路面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers laid a pervious pavement to reduce street flooding.  工程师铺设了透水路面以减少街道积水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impassable</td><td>/ɪmˈpæsəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不能通行的</td><td>an impassable road<br>无法通行的道路</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow made the mountain pass impassable for a week.  大雪使山口一周内无法通行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impenetrable</td><td>/ɪmˈpenɪtrəbl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以理解的</td><td>impenetrable jargon<br>晦涩难懂的行话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract was written in impenetrable jargon that confused everyone.  合同用晦涩难懂的行话写成，让所有人都困惑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impermeable</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrmiəbl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可渗透的</td><td>an impermeable membrane<br>不透水的薄膜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Clay forms an impermeable layer beneath the reservoir.  黏土在水库下方形成了一层不透水的岩层。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impervious</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrviəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可渗透的，不可穿透的；不能被破坏的；不为所动的</td><td>impervious to water<br>不透水的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coating makes the fabric impervious to rain and light oil.  这层涂层使织物不透雨水和轻质油。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>portentous</b></td><td>/pɔːrˈtentəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>预兆性的，凶兆的</td><td>a portentous silence<br>预示不祥的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A portentous silence fell over the room before the verdict was read.  宣读判决之前，房间里陷入一阵预示不祥的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 baleful</td><td>/ˈbeɪlfl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有害的</td><td>a baleful influence<br>有害的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His baleful influence on the young players worried the coach.  他对年轻球员的有害影响让教练担忧。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 direful</del></td><td>/ˈdaɪrfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可怕的，凄惨的</td><td>direful warnings<br>可怕的警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old prophet issued direful warnings about the coming winter.  老先知对将至的冬天发出可怕的警告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doomy</td><td>/ˈduːmi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>凶兆的，阴郁悲观的</td><td>a doomy prediction<br>不祥的预言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Newspapers printed doomy forecasts about the country's economy.  报纸刊出了对国家经济的悲观预测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreboding</td><td>/fɔːrˈboʊdɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>不祥的；不祥的预感</td><td>a sense of foreboding<br>不祥的预感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt a strange foreboding as the sky darkened.  天色转暗时她有种奇怪的不祥预感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inauspicious</td><td>/ˌɪnɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不吉利的，不祥的</td><td>an inauspicious start<br>不吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an inauspicious start by losing twice at home.  球队主场连输两场，开局不利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 menacing</td><td>/ˈmenəsɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>威胁的，凶险的</td><td>a menacing look<br>凶险的眼神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two menacing dogs stood at the end of the narrow lane.  两条凶恶的狗站在窄巷尽头。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 minatory</del></td><td>/ˈmɪnətɔːri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>带来威胁的，有凶兆的</td><td>a minatory tone<br>带威胁的语气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He sent a minatory letter demanding payment within one week.  他寄来一封带威胁的信，要求一周内付款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ominous</td><td>/ˈɑːmɪnəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>凶兆的</td><td>an ominous silence<br>不祥的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An ominous silence fell over the crowd as the announcement began.  宣布开始时，人群陷入不祥的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sinister</td><td>/ˈsɪnɪstər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>阴险的，不祥的</td><td>a sinister smile<br>阴险的笑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was something sinister about the empty old house.  那座空荡的老房子有种阴森之感。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 ill-omened</del></td><td>/ˌɪl ˈoʊmənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不祥的，凶兆的</td><td>an ill-omened journey<br>一次不祥的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Sailors called it an ill-omened voyage from the very start.  水手们从一开始就说这是一次不祥的航行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 auspicious</td><td>/ɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好兆头的</td><td>an auspicious beginning<br>吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain on the wedding day was considered an auspicious beginning.  婚礼当天下雨被认为是个好兆头的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 propitious</td><td>/prəˈpɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吉祥的</td><td>a propitious moment<br>吉利的时机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They waited for a propitious moment to announce the merger.  他们等待一个吉利的时机来宣布合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>poseur</b></td><td>/poʊˈzɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>故作姿态、不真诚的人</td><td>a shameless poseur<br>毫不羞愧的做作者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone at the gallery saw him as a poseur rather than a genuine artist.  画廊里人人都觉得他是个故作姿态的人，而非真正的艺术家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 grandstander</del></td><td>/ˈɡrændˌstændər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>爱哗众取宠的人</td><td>a political grandstander<br>政治上的哗众取宠者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Colleagues dismissed the senator as a mere grandstander.  同僚们把这位参议员看作只会哗众取宠的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 poser</td><td>/ˈpoʊzər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>装样子的人；难题</td><td>just a poser<br>只是个装样子的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The other musicians thought he was just a poser.  其他乐手觉得他只是个装样子的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>posit</b></td><td>/ˈpɑːzɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>假定，断定</td><td>posit a simple rule<br>假定一条简单规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The researchers posit that early humans migrated along the coast.  研究者假定早期人类是沿着海岸迁徙的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assume</td><td>/əˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>就任，承担（职权）；假定</td><td>assume office<br>就职上任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She will assume office as director next Monday.  她将于下周一就任主任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 postulate</td><td>/ˈpɑːstʃələt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>假定，假设</td><td>a basic postulate<br>一个基本假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His theory rests on a single postulate that no one has yet disproved.  他的理论建立在一个至今无人驳倒的假设之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presuppose</td><td>/ˌpriːsəˈpoʊz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预设，以…为前提</td><td>presuppose good faith<br>以诚信为前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument presupposes that everyone acts in good faith.  他的论证预设了每个人都出于诚信行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 premise</td><td>/ˈpremɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>前提，假定</td><td>a false premise<br>错误的前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole plan rests on a false premise about demand.  整个计划建立在一个关于需求的错误前提上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presume</td><td>/prɪˈzuːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，假定；擅自做</td><td>presume innocence<br>假定无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must presume the letter was lost in the post.  我们只能假定那封信在邮寄中丢失了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 falsify</td><td>/ˈfɔːlsəˌfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡改，伪造；证明为假</td><td>falsify the records<br>篡改记录</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An accountant was accused of falsifying the company's records.  一名会计被指控篡改公司账目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>postulate</b></td><td>/ˈpɑːstʃələt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>假定，假设</td><td>a basic postulate<br>一个基本假设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His theory rests on a single postulate that no one has yet disproved.  他的理论建立在一个至今无人驳倒的假设之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 premise</td><td>/ˈpremɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>前提，假定</td><td>a false premise<br>错误的前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole plan rests on a false premise about demand.  整个计划建立在一个关于需求的错误前提上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presumption</td><td>/prɪˈzʌmpʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>冒失无礼；假定</td><td>forgive my presumption<br>请原谅我的冒失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was sheer presumption to speak for the whole committee.  代表整个委员会发言纯属冒失无礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presupposition</td><td>/ˌpriːsəpəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>预设，假定</td><td>an unexamined presupposition<br>未经检验的预设</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her essay attacks an unexamined presupposition of modern economics.  她的文章批判了现代经济学一个未经检验的预设。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supposition</td><td>/ˌsʌpəˈzɪʃn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>猜想，推测</td><td>based on supposition<br>基于猜测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their conclusion rests on supposition rather than on hard evidence.  他们的结论基于猜测，而非确凿证据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assume</td><td>/əˈsuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>就任，承担（职权）；假定</td><td>assume office<br>就职上任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She will assume office as director next Monday.  她将于下周一就任主任。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conjecture</td><td>/kənˈdʒektʃər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>推测，猜测；（没有依据地）认为</td><td>pure conjecture<br>纯属推测</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Without records, any date for the painting is pure conjecture.  没有记录，这幅画的年代纯属推测。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hypothesize</td><td>/haɪˈpɑːθəˌsaɪz/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>假设，提出假说</td><td>hypothesize a link<br>假设存在某种联系</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Researchers hypothesized a link between sleep and memory formation.  研究者假设睡眠与记忆形成之间存在联系。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 posit</td><td>/ˈpɑːzɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>假定，断定</td><td>posit a simple rule<br>假定一条简单规则</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The researchers posit that early humans migrated along the coast.  研究者假定早期人类是沿着海岸迁徙的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presuppose</td><td>/ˌpriːsəˈpoʊz/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预设，以…为前提</td><td>presuppose good faith<br>以诚信为前提</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument presupposes that everyone acts in good faith.  他的论证预设了每个人都出于诚信行事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 presume</td><td>/prɪˈzuːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>推测，假定；擅自做</td><td>presume innocence<br>假定无罪</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We must presume the letter was lost in the post.  我们只能假定那封信在邮寄中丢失了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suppose</td><td>/səˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>猜想，假定，认为</td><td>suppose for a moment<br>姑且假定一下</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Suppose the train is late; how will you reach the airport?  假设火车晚点，你怎么去机场？</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conclusion</td><td>/kənˈkluːʒən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>结束；结论</td><td>bring to a conclusion<br>使结束</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The trial reached its conclusion after eleven long weeks.  审判在漫长的十一周后结束。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>posture</b></td><td>/ˈpɑːstʃər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>装样子；摆姿态作秀</td><td>posture for the cameras<br>对着镜头装模作样</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The candidates merely postured for the cameras instead of debating issues.  候选人只是对着镜头摆姿态，而不辩论议题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 attitudinize</del></td><td>/ˌætəˈtudənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>装模作样，故作姿态</td><td>attitudinize before an audience<br>在观众前故作姿态</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young actor attitudinized instead of simply speaking his lines.  这位年轻演员不老实说台词，反而故作姿态。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 feign</td><td>/feɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>制造假象，装出…的样子</td><td>feign illness<br>装病</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He feigned surprise when they announced the award.  他们宣布获奖时他装出惊讶的样子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grandstand</td><td>/ˈɡrændstænd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>为了给人留下印象而表演，哗众取宠</td><td>grandstand for the cameras<br>对着镜头哗众取宠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The senator was clearly grandstanding rather than negotiating in good faith.  这位参议员显然是在哗众取宠，而不是诚意谈判。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 masquerade</td><td>/ˌmæskəˈreɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>面具，伪装</td><td>a masquerade of concern<br>假装关心的伪装</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His politeness was only a masquerade concealing deep resentment toward his colleagues.  他的客气只是掩盖对同事深深怨恨的伪装。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pose</td><td>/ˈpoʊz/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>假装，冒充；姿态</td><td>pose as an expert<br>冒充专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He posed as a doctor in order to get into the ward.  他冒充医生以便进入病房。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pretend</td><td>/prɪˈtend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>假装，装作</td><td>pretend to be asleep<br>装睡</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children pretended not to hear their mother calling.  孩子们假装没听见母亲呼唤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 2

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>potable</b></td><td>/ˈpoʊtəbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>饮品，尤指有酒精饮料</td><td>a strong potable<br>一种烈性饮品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The innkeeper offered us a strong potable brewed from local barley.  店主给我们端上一种用本地大麦酿的烈性饮品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 beverage</td><td>/ˈbevərɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>饮料，饮品</td><td>a hot beverage<br>一杯热饮</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The airline offers a hot beverage on every morning flight.  该航空公司每趟早班机都提供热饮。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drink</td><td>/ˈdrɪŋk/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>喝，饮用</td><td>drink plenty of water<br>多喝水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You should drink more water during this hot dry week.  这个干热的一周里你该多喝水。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 juice</td><td>/ˈdʒuːs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>果汁，汁液</td><td>fresh orange juice<br>新鲜橙汁</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She drinks fresh orange juice with breakfast every single day.  她每天早餐都喝新鲜橙汁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 liquor</td><td>/ˈlɪkər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>烈酒，酒</td><td>a bottle of liquor<br>一瓶烈酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The store cannot sell liquor before noon on Sundays.  这家店周日中午前不得售卖烈酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spirits</td><td>/ˈspɪrɪts/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>烈酒，蒸馏酒</td><td>a bottle of spirits<br>一瓶烈酒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old cabinet held several dusty bottles of spirits.  那只旧柜子里放着几瓶满是灰的烈酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drinkable</td><td>/ˈdrɪnkəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>可饮用的；饮品</td><td>barely drinkable water<br>勉强可喝的水</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After boiling, the river water became barely drinkable.  煮沸之后，河水勉强可以饮用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 edible</td><td>/ˈedəbl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>可食用的</td><td>edible wild mushrooms<br>可食用的野生蘑菇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guide showed us which edible wild mushrooms grow beneath these pines.  导游告诉我们这些松树下长着哪些可食用的野生蘑菇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 non-intoxicant</td><td>/ˌnɑn ɪnˈtɑksəkənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>不含酒精的饮品</td><td>serve a non-intoxicant<br>提供无酒精饮品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The host offered a non-intoxicant to every driver at the party.  主人给聚会上每位司机都提供了无酒精饮品。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 soft drink</td><td>/sɔft drɪŋk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>软饮料，不含酒精饮料</td><td>a cold soft drink<br>一杯冰的软饮料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He ordered a cold soft drink instead of beer.  他点了一杯冰软饮而不是啤酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>potentate</b></td><td>/ˈpoʊtnteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>有权势的人</td><td>an Eastern potentate<br>一位东方权贵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ancient potentate ruled his desert kingdom with absolute and unquestioned authority.  那位古代权贵以绝对且不容置疑的权威统治他的沙漠王国。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 authority</td><td>/əˈθɔːrəti/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（某领域的）权威人士；管辖权，控制</td><td>an authority on ancient coins<br>古钱币方面的权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is a recognized authority on ancient Greek coins.  她是古希腊钱币方面公认的权威人士。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 autocrat</td><td>/ˈɔːtəˌkræt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>独裁者，专制统治者</td><td>rule like an autocrat<br>像独裁者一样统治</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The founder ran his company like a benevolent autocrat.  创始人像一位仁慈的独裁者那样经营公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 monarch</td><td>/ˈmɑːnərk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>君主，帝王</td><td>an absolute monarch<br>专制君主</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 For forty years the country was ruled by an absolute monarch.  四十年间这个国家由一位专制君主统治。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ruler</td><td>/ˈruːlər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>统治者，君主</td><td>a wise ruler<br>贤明的统治者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a wise and patient ruler.  历史将他记作一位贤明而有耐心的统治者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sovereign</td><td>/ˈsɑːvrən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>拥有最高权力的；君主</td><td>a sovereign state<br>主权国家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The island became a sovereign nation after years of struggle.  经过多年抗争，该岛成为主权国家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 figurehead</td><td>/ˈfɪɡjərˌhed/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>有名无实的首领</td><td>a mere figurehead<br>仅是傀儡首领</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The king became a mere figurehead after the new constitution.  新宪法之后国王成了有名无实的元首。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>potentiate</b></td><td>/poʊˈtenʃɪeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激活，加强</td><td>potentiate the drug's effect<br>增强药物的效力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Alcohol can potentiate the sedative effect of these pills, so avoid drinking.  酒精会加强这些药片的镇静作用，所以要避免饮酒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 activate</td><td>/ˈæktəˌveɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激活，启动</td><td>activate the alarm<br>启动警报</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Smoke from the toaster activated the fire alarm.  烤面包机的烟启动了火警。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 energize</td><td>/ˈenərdʒaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使充满活力，激励</td><td>energize the whole team<br>使整个团队振奋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new coach energized a team that had lost hope.  新教练让失去希望的球队重新振奋起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enhance</td><td>/ɪnˈhæns/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>提高，增强，提升</td><td>enhance the flavor<br>提升风味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A little salt greatly enhances the sweetness of the fruit.  少许盐能大大提升水果的甜味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 intensify</td><td>/ɪnˈtensɪfaɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，激化</td><td>intensify the pressure<br>加大压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain intensified just as we reached the summit.  我们刚到山顶，雨就更大了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wake</td><td>/ˈweɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>醒来，唤醒</td><td>wake up early<br>早早醒来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The noise of traffic woke me before dawn.  车流的噪音在黎明前把我唤醒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 vitalize</td><td>/ˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>赋予活力，激励</td><td>vitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济充满活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New investment vitalized the sleepy town within just two years.  新投资在两年内使这个沉寂的小镇焕发活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deactivate</td><td>/diˈæktɪveɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失效，关闭</td><td>deactivate the alarm<br>关闭警报装置</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A technician deactivated the alarm before entering the vault.  技术员进入金库前关闭了警报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pout</b></td><td>/paʊt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>（尤指撅嘴或板着脸）表示不悦</td><td>pout in silence<br>板着脸不出声</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She began to pout when her parents refused to buy the toy.  父母不肯买那个玩具时，她开始撅嘴表示不悦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 grump</td><td>/ˈɡrʌmp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>生闷气；脾气坏的人</td><td>grump about the weather<br>对天气发牢骚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grumped about the weather all the way to the station.  他一路到车站都在抱怨天气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mope</td><td>/ˈmoʊp/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闷闷不乐，郁闷</td><td>mope around the house<br>在屋里闷闷不乐地转</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She moped around the house for days after the news.  听到消息后她在屋里闷闷不乐地转了好几天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sulk</td><td>/sʌlk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>生气，愠怒</td><td>sulk in silence<br>闷声生气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy sulked in silence for hours after losing the game.  输了比赛后，男孩闷声生气了好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 grin</td><td>/ɡrɪn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>咧嘴笑，咧嘴笑着表示</td><td>grin from ear to ear<br>笑得合不上嘴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grinned broadly when the judges announced his name as the winner.  当评委宣布他获胜时，他开心地咧嘴大笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>practitioner</b></td><td>/prækˈtɪʃənər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>职业人士</td><td>a general practitioner<br>全科医生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The village relies on a single general practitioner for everything.  这个村子事事都依赖唯一的一位全科医生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expert</td><td>/ˈekspərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>专家；熟练的，专业的</td><td>a leading expert<br>顶尖专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They called in an expert to examine the cracked foundation.  他们请来专家检查开裂的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 guru</td><td>/ˈɡuːˌruː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>专家，权威；导师</td><td>a marketing guru<br>营销权威</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They hired a marketing guru to rescue the failing brand.  他们请来一位营销权威拯救这个衰落的品牌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 professional</td><td>/prəˈfeʃənəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>专业的；专业人士</td><td>a professional musician<br>专业的音乐家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only a professional could repair such a delicate mechanism.  只有专业人士才能修好如此精密的机构。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 specialist</td><td>/ˈspeʃələst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>专家，专门研究者</td><td>consult a specialist<br>咨询专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her doctor sent her to a specialist in rare blood diseases.  医生把她转诊给罕见血液病专家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 virtuoso</td><td>/ˌvɜːrtʃuˈoʊsoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>技艺大师，演奏名家；技艺精湛的</td><td>a violin virtuoso<br>小提琴演奏大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violin virtuoso played the difficult passage without a single mistake.  那位小提琴大师毫无差错地演奏了这段难曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fledgling</td><td>/ˈfledʒlɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>新手</td><td>a fledgling company<br>新兴的公司</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The fledgling airline struggled through its very first winter.  这家新兴航空公司艰难度过了第一个冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>premium</b></td><td>/ˈpriːmiəm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>高端的</td><td>premium quality goods<br>高端品质商品</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The shop sells only premium coffee beans from small farms.  这家店只卖来自小农场的高端咖啡豆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expensive</td><td>/ɪkˈspensɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>昂贵的</td><td>an expensive watch<br>一只昂贵的手表</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wore an expensive watch that his grandfather had left him.  他戴着祖父留给他的一只昂贵手表。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extravagant</td><td>/ɪkˈstrævəɡənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的；过度的，不必要的</td><td>extravagant spending<br>挥霍的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their extravagant spending on parties left the charity nearly broke.  他们在宴会上的挥霍开支让这家慈善机构几乎破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precious</td><td>/ˈpreʃəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>珍贵的，贵重的</td><td>precious stones<br>宝石，贵重石料</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The crown was set with precious stones from three continents.  王冠上镶着来自三大洲的宝石。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 costly</td><td>/ˈkɑːstli/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>昂贵的；代价高的</td><td>a costly mistake<br>代价高昂的错误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Ignoring the warning proved a costly mistake for the firm.  忽视警告对这家公司是个代价高昂的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 valuable</td><td>/ˈvæljəbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有价值的，贵重的</td><td>a valuable painting<br>一幅贵重的画</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A valuable painting hung unnoticed in the hallway for years.  一幅贵重的画在走廊里被忽视地挂了多年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prate</b></td><td>/preɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>闲聊，空谈</td><td>prate about nothing<br>空谈毫无内容之事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He would prate for hours about matters he barely understood.  他会就自己几乎不懂的事情空谈好几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 babble</td><td>/ˈbæbl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>发出含糊无意义的嘟囔声，胡乱说</td><td>babble on about nothing<br>没完没了地瞎扯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The toddler would babble happily for hours in his crib.  这个学步的孩子会在婴儿床里开心地咿呀乱说几个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chat</td><td>/ˈtʃæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>闲聊，聊天</td><td>chat over coffee<br>喝咖啡时闲聊</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They chatted over coffee for nearly two pleasant hours.  他们喝着咖啡愉快地闲聊了近两个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 chatter</td><td>/ˈtʃætər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>喋喋不休，闲扯</td><td>chatter endlessly<br>喋喋不休地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The children chattered endlessly during the long bus ride.  孩子们在漫长的巴士行程中喋喋不休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 converse</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>交谈，谈话</td><td>converse in French<br>用法语交谈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two diplomats conversed quietly in French all evening.  两位外交官整晚都用法语轻声交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 gabble</td><td>/ˈɡæbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>急促而含糊地说</td><td>gabble away nervously<br>紧张地急促乱说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The child gabbled excitedly about everything he had seen that day.  孩子兴奋地急促讲述那天见到的一切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 jabber</td><td>/ˈdʒæbər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>快而不清楚地说</td><td>jabber away in Italian<br>用意大利语急促地说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tourists jabbered away in Italian throughout the entire bus ride.  游客们在整段车程中都用意大利语喋喋不休。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prattle</td><td>/ˈprætəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>闲扯，唠叨</td><td>prattle on for hours<br>唠叨好几个小时</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She prattled happily about her garden while we drank our tea.  我们喝茶时她开心地闲扯她的花园。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 twitter</td><td>/ˈtwɪtər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>唧唧喳喳地说；鸟鸣</td><td>twitter with excitement<br>兴奋地喳喳说个不停</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The guests twittered with excitement about the coming wedding.  客人们兴奋地喳喳谈论即将到来的婚礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>preach</b></td><td>/priːtʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>传道，布道</td><td>preach to the congregation<br>向会众布道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young minister will preach in the village chapel every Sunday morning.  这位年轻牧师每周日上午在村里的小教堂布道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discourse</td><td>/dɪsˈkɔːrs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>讲述，论述，谈论</td><td>discourse on morality<br>论述道德问题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The professor discoursed on morality for a full hour.  教授就道德问题论述了整整一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 sermonize</del></td><td>/ˈsɜrmənaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>说教，训诫</td><td>sermonize about duty<br>就责任问题说教</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His uncle loved to sermonize about duty at family dinners.  他叔叔喜欢在家宴上就责任问题说教。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>precarious</b></td><td>/prɪˈkeriəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>危险的，不稳定的；可疑的，不明确的</td><td>a precarious position<br>不稳定的处境</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climbers found themselves in a precarious position on the icy ledge.  攀登者发现自己处在冰封岩架上的危险境地。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 delicate</td><td>/ˈdeləkət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微妙的，精细的；脆弱的</td><td>a delicate distinction<br>微妙的区别</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a delicate ear for the subtle differences between dialects.  她对方言之间的细微差别有很灵敏的耳朵。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fragile</td><td>/ˈfrædʒl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>易碎的</td><td>a fragile vase<br>易碎的花瓶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please pack the fragile vase in plenty of soft paper.  请用足够多的软纸把这个易碎的花瓶包好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sensitive</td><td>/ˈsensətɪv/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>敏感的，灵敏的</td><td>sensitive to light<br>对光敏感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This instrument is sensitive enough to detect the faintest vibration.  这台仪器灵敏到能测出最微弱的震动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 touchy</td><td>/ˈtʌtʃi/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>易怒的，敏感的</td><td>a touchy subject<br>敏感的话题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Money is a touchy subject in that family, so avoid it.  钱在那个家里是敏感话题，别提。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unstable</td><td>/ʌnˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不稳定的，易变的</td><td>an unstable currency<br>不稳定的货币</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ladder felt unstable, so we refused to climb any higher.  梯子感觉不稳，我们不肯再往上爬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ambiguous</td><td>/æmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模糊的，含义不明的</td><td>an ambiguous reply<br>含糊其辞的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract's wording is ambiguous and invites future disputes.  合同措辞含义不明，容易引发日后争端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 doubtful</td><td>/ˈdaʊtfəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以确定的，可疑的</td><td>of doubtful value<br>价值可疑的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is doubtful whether the bridge can carry such loads.  这座桥能否承受如此重量还很难说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 dubious</td><td>/ˈduːbiəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>充满不定性的，值得怀疑的</td><td>a dubious claim<br>可疑的说法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The advertisement made a dubious claim that no serious scientist would support.  那则广告作出了一个没有严肃科学家会支持的可疑说法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 equivocal</td><td>/ɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>模棱两可的，含糊的</td><td>an equivocal answer<br>模棱两可的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The witness gave an equivocal account of that evening.  证人对那晚的叙述模棱两可。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 uncertain</td><td>/ʌnˈsɜːrtn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不确定的，不明朗的</td><td>an uncertain future<br>不明朗的未来</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The date of the meeting remains uncertain until funding is approved.  在资金获批前会议日期仍不确定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unfounded</td><td>/ənˈfaʊndɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无根据的</td><td>unfounded rumors<br>毫无根据的谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company called the reports of bankruptcy completely unfounded.  公司称破产的报道完全没有根据。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 firm</td><td>/ˈfɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>坚定的，坚固的</td><td>a firm decision<br>坚定的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remained firm despite enormous pressure from the board.  尽管董事会施加巨大压力，她依然坚定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 stable</td><td>/ˈsteɪbəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>稳定的；马厩</td><td>a stable income<br>稳定的收入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His condition has been stable since the operation last Tuesday.  自上周二手术以来他的病情一直稳定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 indubitable</td><td>/ɪnˈduːbɪtəbl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不容置疑的</td><td>an indubitable fact<br>不容置疑的事实</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her talent for languages is indubitable.  她的语言天赋是不容置疑的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unambiguous</td><td>/ˌʌnæmˈbɪɡjuəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>明确的，毫不含糊的</td><td>unambiguous written instructions<br>明确的书面指示</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The contract gives unambiguous rules about late payment.  合同就迟付款给出了明确规定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unequivocal</td><td>/ˌʌnɪˈkwɪvəkəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫不含糊的，明确的</td><td>an unequivocal denial<br>毫不含糊的否认</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board issued an unequivocal statement supporting the director.  董事会发表了明确支持主任的声明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unquestionable</td><td>/ənˈkwestʃənəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>毫无疑问的</td><td>unquestionable integrity<br>毋庸置疑的正直</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His loyalty to the team is unquestionable.  他对团队的忠诚毫无疑问。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>precedent</b></td><td>/ˈpresɪdənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>先例；先前的</td><td>set a precedent<br>开创一个先例</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court's ruling set a precedent for all similar cases.  法院的裁决为所有类似案件开创了先例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 example</td><td>/ɪɡˈzæmpəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>范例，典范；例子</td><td>set a good example<br>作出好榜样</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chapel is a fine example of early Gothic building.  这座礼拜堂是早期哥特建筑的绝佳范例。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instance</td><td>/ˈɪnstəns/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>举例说明；例子</td><td>cite a specific instance<br>举出具体例子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She cited a specific instance to support her broad claim.  她举出具体例子支持自己的宏观论断。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 model</td><td>/ˈmɑːdəl/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>典范，模范；模型</td><td>a model of efficiency<br>效率的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic became a model of efficiency for the region.  这家诊所成了全区效率的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 paradigm</td><td>/ˈpærədaɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>典范，模范</td><td>a paradigm of good design<br>优秀设计的典范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This small library is a paradigm of thoughtful modern design.  这座小图书馆是精心现代设计的典范。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pattern</td><td>/ˈpætərn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>范例，样板；模式</td><td>a pattern to follow<br>可效法的样板</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The reform became a pattern for other cities to follow.  这项改革成了其他城市效法的样板。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 standard</td><td>/ˈstændərd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>标准的；标准，规范</td><td>standard procedure<br>标准流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Checking IDs is standard procedure at the entrance.  在入口查验证件是标准流程。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antecedent</td><td>/ˌæntəˈsiːdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>先前的；前事，前情</td><td>antecedent events<br>先前发生的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians study the antecedent causes of the revolution.  历史学家研究这场革命的先前起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 anterior</td><td>/ænˈtɪriər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>前面的</td><td>the anterior part<br>前面的部分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The surgeon examined the anterior surface of the injured knee.  外科医生检查了受伤膝盖的前侧表面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 former</td><td>/ˈfɔːrmər/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>从前的，前任的</td><td>the former president<br>前任总统</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our former neighbor now runs a bakery downtown.  我们从前的邻居现在在市中心开面包店。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preceding</td><td>/priˈsiːdɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>在前的，前面的</td><td>the preceding chapter<br>前面一章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Read the preceding paragraph again before you answer.  回答之前请再读一遍前面那段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 previous</td><td>/ˈpriːviəs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>先前的，之前的</td><td>the previous ruling<br>先前的裁决</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court overturned the previous ruling on similar grounds.  法院以类似理由推翻了先前的裁决。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prior</td><td>/ˈpraɪər/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>先前的，在前的</td><td>prior experience required<br>需要先前的经验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No prior knowledge of coding is needed for this course.  这门课不需要任何先前的编程知识。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ensuing</td><td>/ɪnˈsuːɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>随后的，接着发生的</td><td>the ensuing years<br>随后的几年</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In the ensuing panic, several passengers lost their luggage.  在随后的慌乱中，几位乘客丢了行李。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 3

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>ploy</b></td><td>/ˈplɔɪ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>计策，手段</td><td>a clever marketing ploy<br>巧妙的营销手段</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The free samples were simply a clever marketing ploy.  免费样品只是一个巧妙的营销手段。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 artifice</td><td>/ˈɑːrtəfɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，欺骗；巧妙手段</td><td>use clever artifice<br>使用巧妙的诡计</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her apparent shyness was pure artifice designed to disarm her rivals.  她表面的羞怯纯属伪装，为的是麻痹对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 device</td><td>/dɪˈvaɪs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>手段，策略；装置</td><td>a clever narrative device<br>巧妙的叙事手法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Flashbacks are a device she uses in every novel.  倒叙是她每部小说都用的手法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 knack</td><td>/næk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>诀窍，聪明的做法</td><td>a knack for languages<br>学语言的天分</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My sister has a remarkable knack for learning foreign languages quickly.  我妹妹在快速学外语上有惊人的天分。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trick</td><td>/ˈtrɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>欺骗，诱骗；诡计</td><td>trick someone into paying<br>诱骗某人付钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She tricked him into revealing the password to the account.  她骗他说出了账户密码。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scheme</td><td>/ˈskiːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>阴谋，计划</td><td>a scheme to defraud investors<br>诈骗投资者的阴谋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They devised a scheme to avoid paying any taxes.  他们设计了一个逃税的计谋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stratagem</td><td>/ˈstrætədʒəm/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>谋略，策略</td><td>a military stratagem<br>一项军事谋略</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general won the pass by a simple stratagem.  将军用一个简单的谋略夺取了关隘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wile</td><td>/ˈwaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>诡计，花招</td><td>use every wile<br>使尽各种花招</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She used every wile she knew to win his support.  她用尽一切手段来赢得他的支持。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>precipitate</b></td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flying</td><td>/ˈflaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的；飞行的</td><td>a flying visit<br>匆匆一趟拜访</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She paid us a flying visit between two long meetings.  她在两场长会之间匆匆来看了我们一趟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headlong</td><td>/ˈhedlɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>鲁莽的，草率的</td><td>rush headlong into<br>贸然冲进…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their headlong rush to publish left several errors in the final report.  他们急于发表的草率做法使最终报告留下了几处错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurried</td><td>/ˈhɜːrid/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，仓促的</td><td>a hurried decision<br>仓促做出的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hurried decision to sell cost them a great deal.  他们仓促决定出售，代价惨重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overhasty</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈheɪsti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于匆忙的，草率的</td><td>an overhasty judgment<br>过于草率的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their overhasty decision to expand nearly ruined the young company.  他们草率的扩张决定几乎毁了这家年轻公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitous</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪtəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常陡峭的；匆忙的</td><td>a precipitous decline<br>急剧的下滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path became precipitous near the summit, forcing us to use ropes.  靠近峰顶时山路变得非常陡峭，迫使我们用上绳索。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rash</td><td>/ræʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>草率的，仓促的</td><td>a rash decision<br>草率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He regretted his rash decision to sell the house so quickly.  他后悔自己如此草率地决定迅速卖掉房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rushed</td><td>/ˈrʌʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，草率的</td><td>a rushed decision<br>草率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report felt rushed and full of careless mistakes.  这份报告显得草率，满是粗心的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accelerate</td><td>/əkˈseləreɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>加速，加快</td><td>accelerate the process<br>加快进程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New funding accelerated the development of the vaccine.  新的资金加快了疫苗的研发。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expedite</td><td>/ˈekspədaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加快进程</td><td>expedite the process<br>加快流程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Hiring two more clerks expedited the review of every application.  又雇了两名职员加快了每份申请的审核。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hasten</td><td>/ˈheɪsn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加速，促进；催促</td><td>hasten the decision<br>促使尽快决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm weather hastened the melting of snow on the high mountain passes.  暖和的天气促进了高山山口积雪的融化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impel</td><td>/ɪmˈpel/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>驱使，激励</td><td>impel someone to act<br>驱使某人行动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Curiosity impelled her to open the sealed envelope.  好奇心驱使她打开了密封的信封。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 speed</td><td>/ˈspiːd/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>快速行进，疾驰</td><td>speed down the highway<br>在公路上疾驰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ambulance sped down the empty highway toward the hospital.  救护车沿着空旷的公路疾驰驶向医院。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trigger</td><td>/ˈtrɪɡər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>引发，触发</td><td>trigger a crisis<br>引发一场危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One careless remark triggered a diplomatic crisis that summer.  那年夏天一句失口的话引发了外交危机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 encumber</td><td>/ɪnˈkʌmbər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍；妨碍</td><td>encumber the project<br>妨碍这个项目</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Too many rules encumber small businesses in this country.  太多规定阻碍了这个国家的小企业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 enfetter</del></td><td>/ɛnˈfɛtər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>束缚，使受奴役</td><td>enfetter the human spirit<br>束缚人的精神</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old superstitions still enfetter the minds of many villagers.  旧迷信仍束缚着许多村民的思想。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hamper</td><td>/ˈhæmpər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍；阻碍；束缚</td><td>hamper the rescue effort<br>妨碍救援工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy snow hampered the rescue teams trying to reach the stranded climbers.  大雪妨碍了试图接近被困登山者的救援队。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 handicap</td><td>/ˈhændikæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>妨碍，使不利；障碍</td><td>handicap the team<br>使球队处于不利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor equipment handicapped the researchers from the very start.  设备不足从一开始就妨碍了研究人员。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hinder</td><td>/ˈhɪndər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻碍，妨碍</td><td>hinder progress<br>妨碍进展</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Poor lighting hindered the search for survivors.  照明不足妨碍了对生还者的搜寻。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 manacle</del></td><td>/ˈmænəkl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>给…戴上手铐；束缚，限制</td><td>manacle the prisoner<br>给囚犯戴上手铐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Heavy debts manacle the young couple, leaving them no freedom to change careers.  沉重的债务束缚了这对年轻夫妇，让他们没有转行的自由。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 obstruct</td><td>/əbˈstrʌkt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>妨碍，阻塞</td><td>obstruct justice<br>妨碍司法公正</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fallen tree obstructed the only road out of town.  一棵倒下的树堵住了唯一的出城道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retard</td><td>/rɪˈtɑːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>减速，延迟</td><td>retard the growth<br>延缓生长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Language barriers retarded the progress of the peace negotiations.  语言障碍阻碍了和平谈判的进展。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trammel</td><td>/ˈtræməl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>束缚，限制</td><td>the trammels of custom<br>习俗的束缚</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old rules trammel the creativity of young designers here.  旧规矩束缚了这里年轻设计师的创造力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>precipitation</b></td><td>/prɪˌsɪpɪˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>降水；沉淀物；仓促</td><td>annual precipitation levels<br>年降水量水平</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Annual precipitation in the region has fallen sharply over the past decade.  该地区的年降水量在过去十年里急剧下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deposit</td><td>/dɪˈpɑːzɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>自然积累，沉积，矿藏；存钱</td><td>a deposit of coal<br>一处煤的矿藏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Geologists found a rich deposit of copper beneath the dry valley.  地质学家在干谷下面发现了丰富的铜矿藏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sediment</td><td>/ˈsedəmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>沉淀物，沉积物</td><td>a layer of river sediment<br>一层河流沉积物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thick sediment settled at the bottom of the old reservoir.  厚厚的沉积物沉在旧水库底部。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 haste</td><td>/ˈheɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>急速，匆忙</td><td>in great haste<br>极为匆忙地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 In her haste she left the tickets on the kitchen table.  她匆忙之中把票忘在了厨房桌上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 hastiness</del></td><td>/ˈheɪstinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>匆忙，草率</td><td>regret one's hastiness<br>后悔自己的草率</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He later regretted the hastiness of his angry reply.  他后来后悔自己气头上回信太草率。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hustle</td><td>/ˈhʌsəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>催促；奋力行进</td><td>hustle through the crowd<br>在人群中挤行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She hustled the children into the car before the rain.  她在下雨前把孩子们匆匆赶上车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 precipitousness</del></td><td>/prɪˈsɪpətəsnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>陡峭；仓促轻率</td><td>the precipitousness of the decline<br>下滑的陡然程度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The precipitousness of the market's fall alarmed every investor.  市场跌势之陡令每个投资者惊慌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rush</td><td>/rʌʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>匆忙，急速行动；催促</td><td>rush to the airport<br>赶往机场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed to the airport and barely caught the flight.  他们赶往机场，勉强赶上了航班。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberation</td><td>/dɪˌlɪbəˈreɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>审慎，从容；商议</td><td>act with deliberation<br>从容审慎地行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She moved with slow deliberation through the crowded gallery.  她从容缓慢地穿过拥挤的画廊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>precipitous</b></td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪtəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非常陡峭的；匆忙的</td><td>a precipitous decline<br>急剧的下滑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The path became precipitous near the summit, forcing us to use ropes.  靠近峰顶时山路变得非常陡峭，迫使我们用上绳索。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abrupt</td><td>/əˈbrʌpt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>突然的；言语生硬的</td><td>an abrupt reply<br>生硬的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His abrupt answer ended the conversation before it really began.  他生硬的回答让谈话还没开始就结束了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arduous</td><td>/ˈɑːrdʒuəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>难以做到的，费劲的</td><td>an arduous journey<br>艰苦的旅程</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The climb proved far more arduous than any of them expected.  这次攀登远比他们任何人预想的都费劲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sheer</td><td>/ˈʃɪr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>完全的，纯粹的</td><td>sheer nonsense<br>纯粹的胡说</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 What he wrote about the treaty was sheer nonsense.  他关于那份条约写的全是胡说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 steep</td><td>/stiːp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>浸泡，浸渍</td><td>steep tea leaves in hot water<br>把茶叶泡在热水里</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Steep the dried leaves in boiling water for about ten minutes.  把干叶子在沸水里浸泡约十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cursory</td><td>/ˈkɜːrsəri/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，不注意细节的</td><td>a cursory glance<br>匆匆一瞥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A cursory inspection missed the cracks running along the bridge supports.  一次匆忙的检查漏掉了桥墩上的裂缝。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flying</td><td>/ˈflaɪɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的；飞行的</td><td>a flying visit<br>匆匆一趟拜访</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She paid us a flying visit between two long meetings.  她在两场长会之间匆匆来看了我们一趟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 headlong</td><td>/ˈhedlɔːŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>鲁莽的，草率的</td><td>rush headlong into<br>贸然冲进…</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their headlong rush to publish left several errors in the final report.  他们急于发表的草率做法使最终报告留下了几处错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hurried</td><td>/ˈhɜːrid/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，仓促的</td><td>a hurried decision<br>仓促做出的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their hurried decision to sell cost them a great deal.  他们仓促决定出售，代价惨重。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 overhasty</del></td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈheɪsti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>过于匆忙的，草率的</td><td>an overhasty judgment<br>过于草率的判断</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their overhasty decision to expand nearly ruined the young company.  他们草率的扩张决定几乎毁了这家年轻公司。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precipitate</td><td>/prɪˈsɪpɪteɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>匆忙的；促使，导致</td><td>a precipitate departure<br>匆忙的离去</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His precipitate resignation surprised colleagues who expected a much longer transition.  他匆忙的辞职让原以为过渡期会长得多的同事大吃一惊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rash</td><td>/ræʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>草率的，仓促的</td><td>a rash decision<br>草率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He regretted his rash decision to sell the house so quickly.  他后悔自己如此草率地决定迅速卖掉房子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rushed</td><td>/ˈrʌʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>匆忙的，草率的</td><td>a rushed decision<br>草率的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report felt rushed and full of careless mistakes.  这份报告显得草率，满是粗心的错误。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 flat</td><td>/ˈflæt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调无光的，乏味的</td><td>a flat performance<br>平淡无味的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paint dried to a flat, lifeless gray.  油漆干成了一种无光而死板的灰色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 level</td><td>/ˈlevəl/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>水平的，平的</td><td>on level ground<br>在平地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Pitch the tent on level ground away from the stream.  把帐篷搭在远离溪水的平地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 deliberate</td><td>/dɪˈlɪbərət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>深思熟虑的，故意的</td><td>a deliberate attempt<br>故意的尝试</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The delay was deliberate, designed to weaken our position.  这次延误是故意的，目的是削弱我们的立场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>preclude</b></td><td>/prɪˈkluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先阻止</td><td>preclude further discussion<br>阻止进一步讨论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A lack of funding does not preclude us from starting the project.  资金不足并不能阻止我们启动这个项目。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 avert</td><td>/əˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>避免，防止；转移（目光）</td><td>avert a disaster<br>避免灾难</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Quick action by the crew averted a serious accident.  船员的迅速行动避免了一场严重事故。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 deter</td><td>/dɪˈtɜːr/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>吓住，威慑</td><td>deter potential criminals<br>威慑潜在罪犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Better street lighting may deter thieves from working in this neighborhood.  更好的街道照明或许能威慑小偷不在这一带作案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forestall</td><td>/fɔːrˈstɔːl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先阻止，抢先防止</td><td>forestall criticism<br>预先化解批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She answered early to forestall further criticism.  她提前作答以预先阻止更多批评。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 obviate</td><td>/ˈɑːbvieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>排除，使不必要</td><td>obviate the need for<br>使……不再必要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A short online form would obviate the need for three separate interviews.  一份简短的在线表格就能使三次单独面谈不再必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prevent</td><td>/prɪˈvent/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>阻止，防止</td><td>prevent an accident<br>防止事故发生</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple handwashing prevents the spread of many common infections.  简单的洗手就能防止许多常见感染传播。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stave off</td><td>/ˈsteɪv ˈɔf/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>避开，暂时挡住</td><td>stave off hunger<br>暂时挡住饥饿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A handful of nuts staved off hunger until dinner.  一把坚果挡住了到晚餐前的饥饿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>precursor</b></td><td>/priːˈkɜːrsər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>先驱者，先导</td><td>a precursor to war<br>战争的先兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising tensions along the border proved a precursor to open war.  边境紧张局势加剧，成了公开战争的先兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 foregoer</del></td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌɡoʊər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>先行者，前辈</td><td>a foregoer of reform<br>改革的先行者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This little machine was the foregoer of the modern computer.  这台小机器是现代计算机的先行者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forerunner</td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌrʌnər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>先驱，前身</td><td>a forerunner of modern jazz<br>现代爵士乐的前身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This simple machine was the forerunner of today's personal computer.  这台简单的机器是今天个人电脑的前身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 harbinger</td><td>/ˈhɑːrbɪndʒər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>预兆，先兆；预示者</td><td>a harbinger of spring<br>春天的先兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first robin is often seen as a harbinger of spring.  第一只知更鸟常被视为春天的预兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 herald</td><td>/ˈherəld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>预示，预兆</td><td>herald a new era<br>预示新时代来临</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invention heralded a new era in communication.  这项发明预示着通信新时代的到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outrider</td><td>/ˈaʊtraɪdər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>先驱，前导者</td><td>an outrider of change<br>变革的先驱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first robins are outriders of the coming spring.  第一批旅鸫是春天将至的先驱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 sequela</del></td><td>/sɪˈkwiːlə/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>后遗症，后续结果</td><td>a lasting sequela<br>持久的后遗结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Chronic fatigue was a common sequela of the illness.  慢性疲劳是这种疾病常见的后遗结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 successor</td><td>/səkˈsesər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>继任者，后继者</td><td>name a successor<br>指定一位继任者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chairman named his successor at the annual meeting.  董事长在年会上指定了他的继任者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>predecessor</b></td><td>/ˈpredəsesər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>前任，先辈</td><td>his immediate predecessor<br>他的直接前任</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She inherited several unfinished reforms from her predecessor in the mayor's office.  她从市长办公室的前任那里接手了几项未完成的改革。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ancestor</td><td>/ˈænsestər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>祖先，祖宗</td><td>trace one's ancestors<br>追溯祖先</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her ancestors came to this valley two centuries ago.  她的祖先两百年前来到这个山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 antecedent</td><td>/ˌæntəˈsiːdnt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>先前的；前事，前情</td><td>antecedent events<br>先前发生的事件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Historians study the antecedent causes of the revolution.  历史学家研究这场革命的先前起因。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 foregoer</del></td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌɡoʊər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>先行者，前辈</td><td>a foregoer of reform<br>改革的先行者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This little machine was the foregoer of the modern computer.  这台小机器是现代计算机的先行者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 forerunner</td><td>/ˈfɔːrˌrʌnər/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>先驱，前身</td><td>a forerunner of modern jazz<br>现代爵士乐的前身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This simple machine was the forerunner of today's personal computer.  这台简单的机器是今天个人电脑的前身。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 precursor</td><td>/priːˈkɜːrsər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>先驱者，先导</td><td>a precursor to war<br>战争的先兆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rising tensions along the border proved a precursor to open war.  边境紧张局势加剧，成了公开战争的先兆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 successor</td><td>/səkˈsesər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>继任者，后继者</td><td>name a successor<br>指定一位继任者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chairman named his successor at the annual meeting.  董事长在年会上指定了他的继任者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>predilection</b></td><td>/ˌpredəˈlekʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱，偏好</td><td>a predilection for jazz<br>对爵士乐的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic's predilection for older films colored every review.  这位评论家对老电影的偏爱影响了他的每篇评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affection</td><td>/əˈfekʃən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>喜爱，钟爱；感情</td><td>an affection for old books<br>对旧书的钟爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He kept a lifelong affection for cheap detective novels.  他一生钟爱廉价侦探小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affinity</td><td>/əˈfɪnəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喜好，倾向；相似，密切关系</td><td>an affinity for music<br>对音乐的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a natural affinity for languages and picks them up quickly.  她天生对语言有亲近感，学得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bias</td><td>/ˈbaɪəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>偏见，偏袒</td><td>free from bias<br>没有偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Reviewers were kept anonymous to reduce bias in judging the papers.  评审匿名以减少论文评判中的偏见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disposition</td><td>/ˌdɪspəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，性情</td><td>a disposition to argue<br>爱争辩的倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a natural disposition to question every rule.  她天生有质疑每条规则的倾向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 inclination</td><td>/ˌɪnkləˈneɪʃən/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，意愿；倾斜</td><td>an inclination to agree<br>倾向于同意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She had no inclination to argue with him that evening.  那天晚上她无意与他争辩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 leaning</td><td>/ˈliːnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>倾斜的；倾向</td><td>a leaning tower<br>倾斜的塔</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The leaning wall was propped up with heavy timbers.  倾斜的墙用粗木支撑起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penchant</td><td>/ˈpentʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>嗜好，强烈爱好</td><td>a penchant for luxury<br>对奢华的偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her penchant for dramatic hats made her instantly recognizable.  她对夸张帽子的偏爱让人一眼就认出她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predisposition</td><td>/ˌpriːdɪspəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，易感性</td><td>a genetic predisposition to<br>遗传易感性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some patients have a predisposition to high blood pressure.  有些病人有高血压的易感倾向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 propensity</td><td>/prəˈpensəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，癖好</td><td>a propensity to exaggerate<br>夸大的倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy has a propensity to lose every umbrella he owns.  这男孩总有把伞丢掉的习性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tendency</td><td>/ˈtendənsi/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，趋势</td><td>a tendency to exaggerate<br>夸张的倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a tendency to exaggerate his own part.  他有夸大自己作用的倾向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aversion</td><td>/əˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>a strong aversion to<br>对…强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a lifelong aversion to crowded parties.  他一生都厌恶拥挤的聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 loathing</td><td>/ˈloʊθɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，憎恶</td><td>a deep loathing of cruelty<br>对残忍的深切憎恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He wrote with obvious loathing about the old regime.  他带着明显的憎恶书写旧政权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 nausea</td><td>/ˈnɔːziə/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恶心，厌恶</td><td>a wave of nausea<br>一阵恶心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A wave of nausea swept over him at the smell.  那股气味让他一阵恶心。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repugnance</td><td>/rɪˈpʌɡnəns/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，憎恶</td><td>repugnance at the idea<br>对这想法感到厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She could not hide her repugnance at the smell of the fish market.  她无法掩饰对鱼市气味的厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 repulsion</td><td>/rɪˈpʌlʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感；排斥</td><td>a feeling of repulsion<br>一种厌恶感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The photographs produced repulsion rather than sympathy.  这些照片引起的是厌恶而非同情。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 revulsion</td><td>/rɪˈvʌlʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>feel deep revulsion<br>感到深深的厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers felt deep revulsion at the cruelty described.  读者对书中描写的残酷感到深深厌恶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>preeminent</b></td><td>/priˈemɪnənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>优秀的，重要的</td><td>the preeminent scholar<br>最杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is the preeminent authority on medieval trade routes in Europe.  他是研究欧洲中世纪贸易路线最杰出的权威。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 distinguished</td><td>/dɪˈstɪŋɡwɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>卓越的，杰出的；高贵的</td><td>a distinguished scholar<br>杰出的学者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prize went to a distinguished historian of medieval law.  奖项授予一位杰出的中世纪法律史学家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illustrious</td><td>/ɪˈlʌstriəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>著名的，杰出的</td><td>an illustrious career<br>辉煌杰出的生涯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She retired after an illustrious career in medical research.  在医学研究领域度过杰出的生涯后，她退休了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incomparable</td><td>/ɪnˈkɑːmpərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>无与伦比的</td><td>incomparable beauty<br>无与伦比的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The soprano's voice was incomparable among singers of her generation.  这位女高音的嗓音在同代歌手中无与伦比。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 notable</td><td>/ˈnoʊtəbəl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>显著的；值得注意的</td><td>a notable achievement<br>显著的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There has been a notable drop in traffic accidents this year.  今年交通事故有显著下降。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outstanding</td><td>/ˌaʊtˈstændɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>杰出的，突出的</td><td>an outstanding performance<br>出色的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave an outstanding performance in her very first concert.  她在首场音乐会上表现出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 peerless</td><td>/ˈpɪrlɪs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无与匹敌的</td><td>a peerless performance<br>无与匹敌的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She remains a peerless interpreter of Chopin's nocturnes.  她仍是肖邦夜曲无人能及的演绎者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 superb</td><td>/sʊˈpɜːrb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极好的，卓越的</td><td>a superb performance<br>一场卓越的表演</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The young violinist gave a superb performance that night.  那晚这位年轻小提琴手献上了卓越的表演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supreme</td><td>/səˈpriːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>最高的，至上的</td><td>of supreme importance<br>极其重要</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court holds supreme authority over all such disputes.  该法院对所有此类争议拥有最高裁判权。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unmatchable</td><td>/ʌnˈmætʃəbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无可匹敌的，无与伦比的</td><td>unmatchable skill<br>无可匹敌的技艺</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her unmatchable skill with the brush astonished older painters.  她无可匹敌的画笔技艺令年长画家惊叹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 negligible</td><td>/ˈneɡlədʒəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>微不足道的，可忽略的</td><td>a negligible difference<br>可忽略的差异</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The extra cost is negligible compared with the savings.  与节省的钱相比，额外成本微不足道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 trivial</td><td>/ˈtrɪviəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无足轻重的，不重要的</td><td>a trivial detail<br>无关紧要的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor refused to argue over such a trivial difference in wording.  编辑不愿为措辞上如此无足轻重的差别争论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>preempt</b></td><td>/priˈempt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>预先占有</td><td>preempt the best seats<br>预先占住最好座位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Early arrivals had preempted the best seats near the stage.  早到的人已经预先占住了靠舞台的最好座位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appropriate</td><td>/əˈproʊprieɪt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词、形容词</td><td>私自挪用；适当的</td><td>appropriate public funds<br>挪用公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Officials were accused of appropriating public money for private use.  官员们被指控将公款挪为私用。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arrogate</td><td>/ˈærəˌɡeɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>擅自占用，霸占</td><td>arrogate power to oneself<br>擅自把权力揽于己身</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council arrogated to itself powers belonging to the mayor.  议会擅自把属于市长的权力揽为己有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commandeer</td><td>/ˌkɑːmənˈdɪr/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>强行征用，强占</td><td>commandeer a vehicle<br>强行征用一辆车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Soldiers commandeered every truck in the village that morning.  那天早晨士兵们征用了村里所有的卡车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convert</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改变，转化</td><td>convert heat into energy<br>把热转化为能量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers converted the abandoned warehouse into a bright public library.  工程师们把废弃的仓库改造成了明亮的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expropriate</td><td>/eksˈproʊpriˌeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>征用，没收</td><td>expropriate private land<br>征收私人土地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The state expropriated private land to build the new railway.  国家征收私人土地来修建新铁路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 seize</td><td>/ˈsiːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>领会，抓住（要点）</td><td>seize the main point<br>抓住要点</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Good students quickly seize the main point of a difficult lecture.  好学生能迅速抓住难懂讲座的要点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 usurp</td><td>/juːˈzɜːrp/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>篡夺，篡位</td><td>usurp the throne<br>篡夺王位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general usurped power after the president fled.  总统逃走后，将军篡夺了权力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 4

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>preen</b></td><td>/priːn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>打扮修饰</td><td>preen before the mirror<br>在镜前打扮修饰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The dancer paused to preen before the tall mirror backstage.  那位舞者在后台的高镜前停下来打扮修饰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
