<tr><td>↳近 titanic</td><td>/taɪˈtænɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>巨大的，力量强大的</td><td>a titanic struggle<br>激烈无比的斗争</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two armies fought a titanic battle across the frozen river.  两支军队在冰封的河上展开了一场惊天动地的大战。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tremendous</td><td>/trəˈmendəs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极大的</td><td>a tremendous impact<br>极大的影响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new policy had a tremendous effect on small farmers everywhere.  新政策对各地小农户产生了极大的影响。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 amazing</td><td>/əˈmeɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惊奇的，了不起的</td><td>an amazing coincidence<br>惊人的巧合</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is amazing how quickly the children learned to swim.  孩子们学会游泳之快令人惊奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 astonishing</td><td>/əˈstɑːnɪʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人震惊的，惊人的</td><td>astonishing progress<br>惊人的进步</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made astonishing progress in only three months.  这个团队仅三个月就取得惊人进步。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 astounding</td><td>/əˈstaʊndɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人吃惊的，出乎意料的</td><td>an astounding discovery<br>惊人的发现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an astounding discovery in the desert last summer.  该团队去年夏天在沙漠中有了令人吃惊的发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 awesome</td><td>/ˈɑːsəm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惊叹的；极好的</td><td>an awesome achievement<br>惊人的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The view from the summit was awesome enough to silence the group.  峰顶的景色壮观得让全队都沉默了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fabulous</td><td>/ˈfæbjələs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幻想中的，不真实的；极好的</td><td>fabulous creatures<br>神话中的生物</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Old maps often showed fabulous creatures swimming in distant seas.  古地图上常画着在远海游动的神话生物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 miraculous</td><td>/mɪˈrækjələs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇迹般的，超乎自然的</td><td>a miraculous recovery<br>奇迹般的康复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Doctors called her recovery nothing short of miraculous.  医生称她的康复简直是奇迹。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 portentous</td><td>/pɔːrˈtentəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>预兆性的，凶兆的</td><td>a portentous silence<br>预示不祥的沉默</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A portentous silence fell over the room before the verdict was read.  宣读判决之前，房间里陷入一阵预示不祥的沉默。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 staggering</td><td>/ˈstæɡərɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>惊人的，令人震惊的</td><td>a staggering amount<br>惊人的数额</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company lost a staggering sum in a single trading afternoon.  公司在一个交易下午就亏掉惊人的一笔钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stunning</td><td>/ˈstʌnɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人震惊的；极漂亮的</td><td>a stunning upset<br>令人震惊的逆转</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The underdog scored a stunning victory in the final round.  弱队在最后一轮取得令人震惊的胜利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 pendous</del></td><td>/ˈpɛndəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>stupendous 断行后的后半</td><td>pendous completes stupendous<br>拼合成原词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Joining stu- with pendous restores the original adjective stupendous.  把 stu- 与 pendous 接起来就还原成 stupendous。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sublime</td><td>/səˈblaɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>崇高的；壮丽的；卓越的</td><td>a sublime landscape<br>崇高壮丽的景色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From the summit we watched a sublime landscape unfold below us.  从山顶我们看着一片壮丽的景色在下方展开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 surprising</td><td>/sərˈpraɪzɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人惊讶的，意外的</td><td>a surprising choice<br>令人意外的选择</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It is surprising how little the old town has changed.  这座老城变化之小令人意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 slight</td><td>/slaɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>轻微的，不重要的；轻视，怠慢</td><td>slight her contribution<br>轻视她的贡献</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He felt slighted when nobody mentioned his contribution during the ceremony.  典礼上无人提及他的贡献，他觉得受到了轻视。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>profane</b></td><td>/prəˈfeɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词、形容词</td><td>亵渎</td><td>profane a sacred place<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals profaned the temple by scrawling slogans on its walls.  破坏者在庙墙上乱涂标语，亵渎了圣殿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 defile</td><td>/dɪˈfaɪl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>玷污，污损；亵渎</td><td>defile a sacred site<br>亵渎圣地</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Vandals defiled the ancient temple with paint and broken glass.  破坏者用油漆和碎玻璃亵渎了那座古庙。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 violate</td><td>/ˈvaɪəleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>违反；侵犯；亵渎</td><td>violate a sacred grave<br>亵渎神圣的坟墓</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Looters violated the tombs that villagers had guarded for centuries.  盗墓者亵渎了村民守护数百年的墓葬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 unviolated</del></td><td>/ʌnˈvaɪəleɪtɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>未被侵犯的，神圣的</td><td>an unviolated sanctuary<br>未受侵犯的圣所</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The tomb remained unviolated for nearly three thousand years.  这座墓近三千年未被侵扰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inviolable</td><td>/ˌɪnˈvaɪələbəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不可侵犯的，神圣的</td><td>an inviolable right<br>不可侵犯的权利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Freedom of conscience is treated here as an inviolable principle.  在这里，良心自由被视为不可侵犯的原则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proffer</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːfər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>献出，提供</td><td>proffer an apology<br>主动致歉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He proffered an apology before anyone had a chance to complain.  还没人来得及抱怨，他就主动致歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extend</td><td>/ɪkˈstend/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，扩展</td><td>extend a deadline<br>延长截止期限</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They agreed to extend the deadline by two weeks.  他们同意把截止日期延长两周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 give</td><td>/ˈɡɪv/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>给予；提供</td><td>give a helping hand<br>伸出援手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She gave her old winter coat to the first person who asked.  她把旧冬衣给了第一个开口要的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tender</td><td>/ˈtendər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>温柔的，心软同情的；正式提出</td><td>a tender heart<br>一颗温柔同情的心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a tender heart and cannot bear to see animals suffer.  她心地温软，见不得动物受苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 trot out</td><td>/trɑt ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>端出（老套说法）；炫示</td><td>trot out the same excuse<br>老调重弹</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Politicians trot out the same tired promises every election season.  每逢选举，政客们都端出同样陈旧的承诺。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 retain</td><td>/rɪˈteɪn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>保留，留住</td><td>retain the original title<br>保留原名</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The museum retained the building's original facade during the renovation.  翻修时博物馆保留了建筑的原有正面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 withhold</td><td>/wɪðˈhoʊld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抑制；扣压，不给予</td><td>withhold information<br>隐瞒信息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was accused of withholding key information.  他被指控隐瞒关键信息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proficient</b></td><td>/prəˈfɪʃnt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，精通的</td><td>proficient in Spanish<br>精通西班牙语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 After two years abroad she became proficient in Spanish and Portuguese.  在国外待了两年后，她精通了西班牙语和葡萄牙语。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accomplished</td><td>/əˈkɑːmplɪʃt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>技艺精湛的，有造诣的</td><td>an accomplished pianist<br>技艺精湛的钢琴家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is an accomplished pianist who has toured three continents.  她是位技艺精湛的钢琴家，已在三大洲巡演。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 complete</td><td>/kəmˈpliːt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>完全的，彻底的；完成</td><td>a complete failure<br>彻底的失败</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first attempt was a complete failure.  第一次尝试彻底失败了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expert</td><td>/ˈekspərt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>专家；熟练的，专业的</td><td>a leading expert<br>顶尖专家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They called in an expert to examine the cracked foundation.  他们请来专家检查开裂的地基。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 skilled</td><td>/ˈskɪld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟练的，有技能的</td><td>skilled labor<br>熟练劳动力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The factory struggles to find skilled workers for its new assembly line.  工厂难以为新装配线找到熟练工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 versed</td><td>/vɜːrst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>熟知的；精通的</td><td>well versed in history<br>精通历史</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He is well versed in both classical and modern poetry.  他对古典诗歌和现代诗歌都很精通。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 virtuoso</td><td>/ˌvɜːrtʃuˈoʊsoʊ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>技艺大师，演奏名家；技艺精湛的</td><td>a violin virtuoso<br>小提琴演奏大师</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The violin virtuoso played the difficult passage without a single mistake.  那位小提琴大师毫无差错地演奏了这段难曲。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inept</td><td>/ɪˈnept/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>愚笨的，荒谬的；不称职的，无能力的</td><td>politically inept<br>政治上无能</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager was so inept at handling complaints that customers left in droves.  这位经理处理投诉如此无能，导致顾客大量流失。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incompetent</td><td>/ˌɪnˈkɑːmpətənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无能的；不称职的</td><td>an incompetent manager<br>无能的管理者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The report blamed an incompetent contractor for the collapsed roof.  报告把屋顶坍塌归咎于一个不称职的承包商。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 amateur</td><td>/ˈæməˌtɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>业余爱好者；外行的</td><td>a talented amateur<br>有天赋的业余者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The comet was first spotted by an amateur astronomer.  这颗彗星最先由一位业余天文学家发现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inexperienced</td><td>/ˌɪnɪkˈspɪriənst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏经验的，不熟练的</td><td>an inexperienced driver<br>缺乏经验的司机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team was young and inexperienced but full of energy.  这支队伍年轻缺乏经验，却充满活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inexpert</td><td>/ˌɪnˈekspɜːrt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，外行的</td><td>inexpert hands<br>不熟练的手法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 To inexpert eyes the two paintings looked exactly the same.  在外行眼里，这两幅画看起来完全一样。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 jackleg</del></td><td>/ˈdʒækˌlɛɡ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>外行的；蹩脚的（人）</td><td>a jackleg carpenter<br>蹩脚的木匠</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some jackleg plumber installed all these pipes backwards years ago.  多年前某个蹩脚水管工把这些管子全装反了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unprofessional</td><td>/ˌʌnprəˈfeʃənəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不专业的，外行的</td><td>unprofessional conduct<br>不专业的行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Arriving late to every meeting looks deeply unprofessional.  每次会议都迟到显得极不专业。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unseasoned</td><td>/ʌnˈsizənd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏经验的；未调味的</td><td>unseasoned recruits<br>毫无经验的新兵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team fielded unseasoned players against the reigning champions.  球队派出毫无经验的球员迎战卫冕冠军。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unskilled</td><td>/ənˈskɪld/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不熟练的，无技术的</td><td>unskilled labor<br>非技术劳工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Most of these jobs are unskilled and pay barely above the minimum.  这些工作多为非技术岗，薪水刚过最低标准。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>profligate</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːflɪɡət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>挥金如土的，挥霍的</td><td>profligate spending<br>挥霍无度的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council's profligate spending on ceremonies angered local taxpayers.  议会在典礼上挥霍无度的开支激怒了当地纳税人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 extravagant</td><td>/ɪkˈstrævəɡənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥霍的；过度的，不必要的</td><td>extravagant spending<br>挥霍的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their extravagant spending on parties left the charity nearly broke.  他们在宴会上的挥霍开支让这家慈善机构几乎破产。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 high-rolling</td><td>/ˈhaɪ ˈroʊlɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>挥金如土的</td><td>a high-rolling lifestyle<br>挥金如土的生活</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His high-rolling habits vanished the moment the money ran out.  钱一花光，他挥金如土的习惯立刻消失了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spendthrift</td><td>/ˈspendθrɪft/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>挥霍者，败家子</td><td>a reckless spendthrift<br>肆意挥霍的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A reckless spendthrift, he owed money to nearly every friend.  作为一个挥霍无度的人，他几乎欠了每个朋友的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 squandering</td><td>/ˈskwɑːndərɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>浪费的，挥霍的</td><td>squandering public funds<br>挥霍公款</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Squandering money on gadgets left him broke by December.  在小玩意上挥霍钱财让他十二月就身无分文。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 thriftless</del></td><td>/ˈθrɪftləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的，浪费的</td><td>thriftless habits<br>浪费的习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His thriftless spending soon exhausted the family savings.  他不节俭的花销很快耗尽了家庭储蓄。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 unthrifty</del></td><td>/ʌnˈθrɪfti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的，挥霍的</td><td>an unthrifty heir<br>挥霍的继承人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The unthrifty young lord sold off the estate piece by piece.  这位挥霍的年轻贵族把地产一块块卖掉了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wasteful</td><td>/ˈweɪstfəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>浪费的，挥霍的</td><td>wasteful of resources<br>浪费资源的</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Printing every email is wasteful and completely unnecessary.  把每封邮件都打印出来既浪费又完全没必要。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 fritterer</del></td><td>/ˈfrɪtərər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>浪费时间或金钱的人</td><td>a hopeless fritterer<br>无可救药的挥霍者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her brother is a fritterer who wastes both his savings and his talent.  她弟弟是个挥霍者，既浪费积蓄也浪费才华。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 highroller</del></td><td>/ˈhaɪ ˌroʊlər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>挥金如土的人；豪赌客</td><td>a Las Vegas highroller<br>拉斯维加斯豪客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The casino treats every highroller to a free suite and private car.  赌场为每位豪客提供免费套房和专车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 spender</td><td>/ˈspendər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>花钱的人；挥霍者</td><td>a big spender<br>花钱大手的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 My uncle is a big spender who never checks his bank balance.  我叔叔花钱大手大脚，从不看银行余额。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 squanderer</del></td><td>/ˈskwɑndərər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>浪费者，挥霍者</td><td>a squanderer of talent<br>浪费才华的人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 History remembers him as a squanderer of a vast family fortune.  历史把他记作败掉万贯家财的挥霍者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 waster</td><td>/ˈweɪstər/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>挥霍者；无用之人</td><td>an idle waster<br>游手好闲的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His father called him a waster who had thrown away every chance.  他父亲说他是个败家子，白白丢掉了每个机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wastrel</td><td>/ˈweɪstrəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>肆意挥霍的人，败家子</td><td>a drunken wastrel<br>酗酒的败家子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The family fortune was spent by a wastrel who gambled nightly.  家族财产被一个夜夜赌博的败家子花光了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 parsimonious</td><td>/ˌpɑːrsəˈmoʊniəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的，过分节俭的</td><td>parsimonious with praise<br>极少给出赞扬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The parsimonious landlord refused to replace the broken heater.  吝啬的房东拒绝更换坏了的暖气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 provident</td><td>/ˈprɑːvɪdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的；有远见的</td><td>a provident household<br>节俭有远见的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A provident household sets money aside long before hard times arrive.  节俭有远见的家庭远在困难到来之前就存下钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 thrift</td><td>/ˈθrɪft/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>节俭，节约</td><td>practice thrift<br>实行节俭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wartime thrift taught my grandmother to save every scrap of string.  战时的节俭让我祖母连每根线头都留着。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 economical</td><td>/ˌekəˈnɑːmɪkəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节约的，经济的</td><td>an economical car<br>省油的汽车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small diesel engine is more economical on long journeys.  小型柴油发动机长途出行更省油。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 frugal</td><td>/ˈfruːɡl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的；节省的；朴素的</td><td>a frugal lifestyle<br>节俭的生活方式</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They led a frugal life and saved almost every coin.  他们过着节俭的生活，几乎每一分钱都存起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 conserving</td><td>/kənˈsɜːrvɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节约的，保存的</td><td>conserving energy<br>节约能源</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple habits like conserving water reduce the monthly bills.  节约用水这类简单习惯能降低每月账单。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>pushover</b></td><td>/ˈpʊʃoʊvər/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>容易做的工作；易如反掌的事</td><td>a complete pushover<br>轻而易举的事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exam looked hard but turned out to be a complete pushover.  那场考试看着难，结果却是易如反掌的事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 breeze</td><td>/ˈbriːz/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻而易举的事；微风</td><td>a breeze to finish<br>轻松就能做完</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The final exam was a breeze compared with the weekly quizzes.  与每周的小测相比，期末考试轻松得多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 cake</td><td>/ˈkeɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>易如反掌的事；蛋糕</td><td>a piece of cake<br>小菜一碟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Fixing that bug turned out to be a piece of cake.  修那个程序缺陷结果只是小菜一碟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 picnic</td><td>/ˈpɪkˌnɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>轻松愉快的事；野餐</td><td>no picnic at all<br>绝不是轻松事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Moving house in the July heat was no picnic for any of us.  七月酷热中搬家对我们谁都不轻松。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snap</td><td>/ˈsnæp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>咬，猛咬；断裂</td><td>snap at the hand<br>猛咬手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frightened dog snapped at the stranger's hand.  受惊的狗猛咬陌生人的手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 headache</td><td>/ˈheˌdeɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>令人头疼的事；头痛</td><td>a real headache<br>十足的麻烦事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Coordinating six time zones became a daily headache for the team.  协调六个时区成了团队每天的头疼事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 labor</td><td>/ˈleɪbər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>劳动，苦工；劳工</td><td>manual labor<br>体力劳动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of hard labor had bent his back.  多年的苦力劳动使他驼了背。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pain</td><td>/ˈpeɪn/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>麻烦事；疼痛</td><td>a pain in the neck<br>讨厌的麻烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Filling out these forms is a pain, but skipping them costs more.  填这些表格很烦人，但不填代价更大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>profusion</b></td><td>/prəˈfjuːʒn/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>a profusion of flowers<br>繁花盛开</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A profusion of wildflowers covered the hillside after the spring rains.  春雨过后，山坡上开满了大量野花。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abundance</td><td>/əˈbʌndəns/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>丰富，大量</td><td>an abundance of fresh fruit<br>大量新鲜水果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The market offered an abundance of fresh fruit at low prices.  市场以低价供应大量新鲜水果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mass</td><td>/ˈmæs/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，大批</td><td>a mass of documents<br>一大堆文件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A mass of documents buried the clerk's small desk.  一大堆文件埋住了那位职员的小书桌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 plentitude</del></td><td>/ˈplɛnɪtud/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>充足，大量</td><td>a plentitude of talent<br>人才济济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The region enjoys a plentitude of water and fertile soil.  该地区拥有充足的水源和肥沃的土壤。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 scads</td><td>/skædz/</td><td>☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>许多，大量</td><td>scads of money<br>大把的钱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The film made scads of money in its first weekend alone.  这部电影仅首个周末就赚了大把的钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 volume</td><td>/ˈvɑːljum/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量；容量；卷</td><td>a great volume of traffic<br>巨大的车流量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The bridge cannot handle this volume of traffic.  这座桥承受不了如此大的车流量。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 wealth</td><td>/ˈwelθ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>大量，丰富；财富</td><td>a wealth of experience<br>丰富的经验</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old editor brought a wealth of experience to the team.  这位老编辑给团队带来了丰富的经验。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 paucity</td><td>/ˈpɔːsəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>少量，缺乏</td><td>a paucity of data<br>数据匮乏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The paucity of reliable data made the researchers cautious about conclusions.  可靠数据的匮乏使研究者对结论十分谨慎。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 scanty</td><td>/ˈskænti/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏的，稀少的</td><td>scanty evidence<br>证据不足</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Evidence for the theory remains scanty.  支持该理论的证据仍然很少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prohibitive</b></td><td>/prəˈhɪbətɪv/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>（价格高得）抑制购买的</td><td>prohibitive prices<br>高得吓人的价格</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The cost of housing here is prohibitive for most young families.  这里的房价对多数年轻家庭来说高不可攀。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proliferate</b></td><td>/prəˈlɪfəreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>快速繁殖；激增</td><td>proliferate rapidly online<br>在网上迅速激增</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cheap smartphones proliferated across the country in just five years.  廉价智能手机在短短五年内遍布全国。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 balloon</td><td>/bəˈluːn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>迅速增加</td><td>costs have ballooned<br>成本迅速膨胀</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repair costs ballooned from two thousand to twenty thousand dollars.  维修费用从两千美元猛增到两万美元。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 boom</td><td>/ˈbuːm/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词、不及物动词</td><td>繁荣，激增；轰鸣</td><td>an economic boom<br>经济繁荣期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tourism boomed after the new airport opened last spring.  新机场去年春天启用后，旅游业迅速繁荣。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 build up</td><td>/bɪld ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>积聚，逐渐增强</td><td>build up pressure<br>积聚压力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic builds up quickly on this road after five o'clock.  五点以后这条路上的车流迅速积聚。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 escalate</td><td>/ˈeskəleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）（战争等）升级，扩大</td><td>escalate into violence<br>升级为暴力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border dispute quickly escalated into a full-scale armed conflict.  边界争端迅速升级为全面武装冲突。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 expand</td><td>/ɪkˈspænd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>扩大，扩张；详述</td><td>expand the business<br>扩大业务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company plans to expand into three new markets.  公司计划扩张到三个新市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mushroom</td><td>/ˈmʌʃruːm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>蘑菇；迅速增长</td><td>mushroom into a crisis<br>迅速演变成危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Small online shops mushroomed across the country within two years.  小型网店两年内在全国迅速涌现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 snowball</td><td>/ˈsnoʊˌbɔːl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>迅速增长，滚雪球般扩大</td><td>snowball into a crisis<br>演变成一场危机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small complaint snowballed into a national controversy.  一个小小的投诉迅速演变成全国性争议。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decrease in amount</td><td>/dɪˈkris ɪn əˈmaʊnt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>数量减少</td><td>decrease in amount sharply<br>数量急剧减少</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rainfall continued to decrease in amount over three consecutive dry summers.  连续三个干旱的夏天里降雨量持续减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dwindle</td><td>/ˈdwɪndl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>逐渐减少</td><td>dwindle to nothing<br>逐渐减少至无</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their savings dwindled during the months of unemployment.  失业的几个月里他们的积蓄逐渐减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 contract</td><td>/ˈkɑːnˌtrækt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>契约，合同；收缩；感染疾病</td><td>sign a contract<br>签订合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both parties signed the contract without reading the final clause carefully.  双方都没有仔细读最后一条就签了合同。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 decrease</td><td>/dɪˈkriːs/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>减少，下降</td><td>a sharp decrease<br>急剧下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Traffic accidents decreased noticeably after the new speed limits appeared.  新限速出台后交通事故明显减少。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 diminish</td><td>/dɪˈmɪnɪʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）减少；轻视，贬低</td><td>diminish in importance<br>重要性下降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His influence diminished steadily after he left the board.  他离开董事会后影响力持续减弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 lessen</td><td>/ˈlesən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>减少，减轻</td><td>lessen the risk<br>减少风险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Simple exercises lessen the pain in his stiff shoulder.  简单的锻炼减轻了他僵硬肩膀的疼痛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 recede</td><td>/rɪˈsiːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>退去，消退，减弱</td><td>recede into the distance<br>渐渐远去消失</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The floodwaters slowly receded from the village streets.  洪水缓缓从村庄的街道退去。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wane</td><td>/weɪn/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词、名词</td><td>减少，衰退</td><td>wax and wane<br>盈亏消长，兴衰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Public interest in the scandal waned within a month.  公众对这桩丑闻的兴趣一个月内就减退了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 8

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prolix</b></td><td>/ˈproʊlɪks/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，冗长的</td><td>prolix legal prose<br>冗长的法律文字</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge criticized the prolix brief for burying its argument.  法官批评这份冗长的书状埋没了自己的论点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 diffuse</td><td>/dɪfˈjuːs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长散漫的；扩散的</td><td>a diffuse and wordy essay<br>散漫冗长的文章</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His argument was far too diffuse to convince a careful reader.  他的论证过于散漫，无法说服细心的读者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 garrulous</td><td>/ˈɡærələs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>啰嗦的，话多令人厌烦的</td><td>a garrulous neighbor<br>唠叨的邻居</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Our garrulous neighbor talked for an hour about his lawn.  我们唠叨的邻居就他的草坪讲了一个小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rambling</td><td>/ˈræmblɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>漫无目的的，散漫的</td><td>a rambling explanation<br>散漫的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He gave a rambling answer that satisfied nobody present.  他给出散漫的回答，在场无人满意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 verbose</td><td>/vɜːrˈboʊs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的，啰嗦的</td><td>a verbose explanation<br>冗长的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her verbose report could have said the same thing in two pages.  她冗长的报告本可以用两页说完同样的内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 windy</td><td>/ˈwɪndi/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>冗长的</td><td>a windy speech<br>冗长空洞的演讲</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His windy speech said very little in more than an hour.  他冗长的演讲讲了一个多小时却几乎没什么内容。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 pithy</td><td>/ˈpɪθi/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简练的，言简意赅的</td><td>a pithy remark<br>精练的评论</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her pithy summary captured the whole debate in two sentences.  她简练的总结用两句话概括了整场辩论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 taciturn</td><td>/ˈtæsɪtɜːrn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>沉默寡言的，话少的</td><td>a taciturn farmer<br>沉默寡言的农夫</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The taciturn farmer answered our questions with a nod or a shrug.  沉默寡言的农夫只用点头或耸肩回答我们的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 terse</td><td>/tɜːrs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简洁的，简明的</td><td>a terse reply<br>简洁的回答</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His terse note said only that the deal had collapsed.  他简短的字条只说交易破裂了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 succinct</td><td>/səkˈsɪŋkt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a succinct summary<br>简洁的总结</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her succinct summary covered the entire report in three short paragraphs.  她简洁的总结用三小段概括了整份报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concise</td><td>/kənˈsaɪs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>简明的，简洁的</td><td>a concise explanation<br>简明的解释</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Keep your answer concise; the interviewer has only ten minutes.  回答要简洁，面试官只有十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extremely brief</td><td>/ɪkˈstrimli ˈbrif/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>极其简短的</td><td>an extremely brief reply<br>极简短的答复</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His answer was extremely brief, just two words and a shrug.  他的回答极其简短，只有两个词加一个耸肩。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prologue</b></td><td>/ˈproʊlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序言</td><td>the prologue to the play<br>该剧的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The prologue to the play explains how the bitter feud began.  该剧的序言说明了这场激烈仇怨的由来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 exordium</td><td>/ɛɡˈzɔrdiəm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>开场白，绪言</td><td>a brief exordium<br>简短的开场白</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The speaker began with a brief exordium praising his hosts.  演讲者以一段简短开场白称赞主办方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreword</td><td>/ˈfɔːrwɜːrd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>前言</td><td>write the foreword<br>撰写前言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A famous historian wrote the foreword to her first book.  一位著名历史学家为她的第一本书撰写了前言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preamble</td><td>/ˈpriːæmbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>序言，开场白</td><td>the preamble to the treaty<br>条约的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The preamble to the treaty states its guiding principles.  条约的序言阐明了其指导原则。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 preface</td><td>/ˈprefəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序言</td><td>write a preface<br>写序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author used the preface to thank everyone who helped her research.  作者在序言中感谢了所有帮助她做研究的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prelude</td><td>/ˈpreɪˌluːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>序曲；前奏，先兆</td><td>a prelude to war<br>战争的前奏</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The border clashes proved to be a prelude to full-scale war.  边境冲突后来成了全面战争的前奏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 proem</del></td><td>/ˈproʊɛm/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>序言，前言</td><td>a short proem<br>简短的序言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The author's proem explains why the book took twenty years.  作者的序言解释了这本书为何写了二十年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 prolusion</del></td><td>/proʊˈluʒən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>序言；开场之作</td><td>serve as a prolusion<br>充当引言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 This essay is only a prolusion to a much longer study.  这篇文章只是一部长篇研究的引言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 epilogue</td><td>/ˈepɪlɔːɡ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>文学作品的结局</td><td>a brief epilogue<br>简短的结语</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A brief epilogue tells the reader what became of the two brothers.  简短的结语告诉读者两兄弟后来的境况。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prolong</b></td><td>/prəˈlɔːŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖延</td><td>prolong someone's life<br>延长某人寿命</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New treatments can prolong life by several years.  新疗法能把生命延长数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elongate</td><td>/ɪˈlɔːŋɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>拉长，延长</td><td>elongate the shape<br>拉长形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tall mirrors elongate the room and make it look larger.  高镜子拉长了房间，使它显得更大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lengthen</td><td>/ˈleŋθən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>变长，加长</td><td>lengthen a skirt<br>把裙子放长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The days lengthen noticeably after the spring equinox.  春分之后白天明显变长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 outstretch</del></td><td>/aʊtsˈtretʃ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>伸展，展开；超过</td><td>outstretch both arms<br>伸开双臂</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The eagle outstretched its wings and glided over the valley.  雄鹰展开双翼，滑过山谷。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 protract</del></td><td>/prəˈtrækt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖长</td><td>protract the negotiations<br>拖长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless objections protracted the meeting into the evening.  无休止的反对把会议拖到了晚上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stretch</td><td>/ˈstretʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，拉长</td><td>stretch out the meeting<br>把会议拖长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They stretched the two-day trip into a leisurely full week.  他们把两天的行程拉长成悠闲的一整周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drag</td><td>/ˈdræɡ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>累赘，阻力；拖曳</td><td>a drag on growth<br>增长的阻力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 High debt became a drag on the country's economy.  高额债务成了该国经济的阻力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abbreviate</td><td>/əˈbriːvieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩写，缩短</td><td>abbreviate a long title<br>缩写长标题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You may abbreviate 'Doctor' to 'Dr.' on the envelope.  信封上可以把 Doctor 缩写为 Dr.。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abridge</td><td>/əˈbrɪdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩短，删节</td><td>an abridged edition<br>删节版</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The publisher abridged the long novel for younger readers.  出版商为年轻读者删节了这部长篇小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 curtail</td><td>/kɜːrˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缩短，削减</td><td>curtail public spending<br>削减公共开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bad weather curtailed our trip by three days.  坏天气使我们的旅程缩短了三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cut</td><td>/ˈkʌt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>缩短；削减</td><td>cut the speech short<br>缩短讲话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The director cut twenty minutes from the film before its release.  导演在影片上映前剪掉了二十分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cut back</td><td>/kʌt ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>削减，缩减</td><td>cut back on sugar<br>减少吃糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had to cut back on spending after the pay cut.  减薪后我们不得不削减开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shorten</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>缩短，删减</td><td>shorten the report<br>删短报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor shortened his rambling article by half.  编辑把他冗长的文章删短了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 truncate</td><td>/ˈtrʌŋkeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>截短，缩短</td><td>truncate a long sentence<br>截短长句</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor truncated the article to fit the page.  编辑把文章截短以适应版面。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>promulgate</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːməlɡeɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>正式宣布</td><td>promulgate a new law<br>颁布新法律</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The government will promulgate a new law on data privacy next month.  政府将于下月正式颁布一项新的数据隐私法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 annunciate</del></td><td>/əˈnʌnsieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣布，宣告</td><td>annunciate the verdict<br>宣布判决结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Trumpets annunciated the arrival of the queen at the palace gate.  号角宣告女王已抵达宫门。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 declare</td><td>/dɪˈkler/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>宣布，声明，断言</td><td>declare one's intentions<br>表明自己的意图</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The judge declared the earlier verdict invalid and ordered a retrial.  法官宣布先前判决无效并下令重审。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enunciate</td><td>/ɪˈnʌnsieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>动词、及物动词</td><td>清晰地说；公开宣布，宣称</td><td>enunciate each word clearly<br>清晰吐出每个词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please enunciate each word clearly so the people at the back can hear.  请把每个词清晰地吐出，好让后排的人听清。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proclaim</td><td>/prəˈkleɪm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣告，使…公之于众</td><td>proclaim independence<br>宣布独立</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The colony voted to proclaim independence from the empire that summer.  那年夏天，殖民地投票宣告脱离帝国独立。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 publicize</td><td>/ˈpʌblɪsaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣传，公布</td><td>publicize the event<br>宣传这场活动</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Organizers publicized the concert with posters across the whole campus.  组织者用海报在全校宣传这场音乐会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 herald</td><td>/ˈherəld/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>预示，预兆</td><td>herald a new era<br>预示新时代来临</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The invention heralded a new era in communication.  这项发明预示着通信新时代的到来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 keep secret</td><td>/kiːp ˈsiːkrət/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>保守秘密，隐瞒</td><td>keep the plan secret<br>把计划保密</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They kept the engagement secret from their families for months.  他们对家人隐瞒了订婚的事好几个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proofread</b></td><td>/ˈpruːfriːd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>校对</td><td>proofread the manuscript<br>校对手稿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please proofread the manuscript carefully before sending it to the printer.  请在送印之前仔细校对这份手稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>propagate</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːpəɡeɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>传播，宣传</td><td>propagate new ideas<br>传播新思想</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Missionaries traveled far to propagate their beliefs among distant communities.  传教士远行到偏远社区去传播他们的信仰。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 broadcast</td><td>/ˈbrɔːdˌkæst/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>广播，播送；散布</td><td>broadcast the news<br>播报消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The station broadcast the news of the rescue every hour.  电台每小时播报一次营救消息。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 circulate</td><td>/ˈsɜːrkjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>散布，传播；流通</td><td>circulate a rumor<br>散播谣言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rumors of a merger circulated quickly among the staff.  合并的传言在员工中迅速传开。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disseminate</td><td>/dɪˈsemɪneɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>传播，散布（信息）</td><td>disseminate new research<br>传播新的研究成果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The society disseminated the findings to schools across the country.  学会把研究结果传播到全国各地的学校。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 check</td><td>/tʃek/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使突然停止，阻止；检查，核对</td><td>check the spread of disease<br>阻止疾病蔓延</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Strict quarantine measures checked the spread of the disease.  严格的隔离措施阻止了疾病的蔓延。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>propensity</b></td><td>/prəˈpensəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，癖好</td><td>a propensity to exaggerate<br>夸大的倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy has a propensity to lose every umbrella he owns.  这男孩总有把伞丢掉的习性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 affinity</td><td>/əˈfɪnəti/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>喜好，倾向；相似，密切关系</td><td>an affinity for music<br>对音乐的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has a natural affinity for languages and picks them up quickly.  她天生对语言有亲近感，学得很快。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 aptitude</td><td>/ˈæptəˌtuːd/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>天资，天生的倾向</td><td>an aptitude for languages<br>语言天赋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed an early aptitude for languages and mastered three by twelve.  她很早显出语言天赋，十二岁前就掌握了三门语言。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bent</td><td>/ˈbent/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>爱好，天生的倾向</td><td>a bent for numbers<br>对数字的天赋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 From childhood he showed a bent for mechanical things.  他从小就显示出对机械的爱好。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 partiality</td><td>/ˌpɑːrʃiˈæləti/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱；偏袒</td><td>a partiality for sweets<br>对甜食的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The coach was accused of partiality toward his own students.  教练被指责偏袒自己的学生。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 penchant</td><td>/ˈpentʃənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>嗜好，强烈爱好</td><td>a penchant for luxury<br>对奢华的偏好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her penchant for dramatic hats made her instantly recognizable.  她对夸张帽子的偏爱让人一眼就认出她。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predilection</td><td>/ˌpredəˈlekʃən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>偏爱，偏好</td><td>a predilection for jazz<br>对爵士乐的偏爱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The critic's predilection for older films colored every review.  这位评论家对老电影的偏爱影响了他的每篇评论。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predisposition</td><td>/ˌpriːdɪspəˈzɪʃən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>倾向，易感性</td><td>a genetic predisposition to<br>遗传易感性</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Some patients have a predisposition to high blood pressure.  有些病人有高血压的易感倾向。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proclivity</td><td>/prəˈklɪvəti/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>癖性，偏好（多指不良倾向）</td><td>criminal proclivities<br>犯罪倾向</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His proclivity for gambling eventually ruined the family.  他的赌博癖好最终毁了这个家。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 aversion</td><td>/əˈvɜːrʒən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>厌恶，反感</td><td>a strong aversion to<br>对…强烈厌恶</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He has a lifelong aversion to crowded parties.  他一生都厌恶拥挤的聚会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prophetic</b></td><td>/prəˈfetɪk/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预言的，预示的</td><td>prophetic words<br>有预言性的话</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His warnings about the flood proved strangely prophetic a year later.  他关于洪水的警告一年后显得出奇地有预言性。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 predictive</td><td>/prɪˈdɪktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>预言的，预测性的</td><td>predictive power<br>预测的能力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The model has strong predictive power for winter storms.  这个模型对冬季风暴有很强的预测能力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>propitiate</b></td><td>/prəˈpɪʃieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>抚慰，劝解</td><td>propitiate the angry gods<br>抚慰愤怒的神灵</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Villagers offered rice and flowers to propitiate the mountain spirits.  村民献上米和花来抚慰山神。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 appease</td><td>/əˈpiːz/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；满足</td><td>appease the angry crowd<br>安抚愤怒的人群</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered refunds to appease its angry customers.  公司提供退款以安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 assuage</td><td>/əˈsweɪdʒ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缓和，减轻；平息</td><td>assuage his fears<br>减轻他的恐惧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's speech did little to assuage public anger.  市长的演讲几乎没能平息公众的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 conciliate</td><td>/kənˈsɪlieɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>安抚，平息；调解</td><td>conciliate angry customers<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The manager tried to conciliate the angry customers with refunds.  经理试图用退款来安抚愤怒的顾客。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 disarm</td><td>/dɪsˈɑːrm/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使息怒，消除敌意；解除武装</td><td>disarm his critics<br>平息批评者的怒气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His frank apology disarmed the critics who had come prepared to attack him.  他坦诚的道歉平息了那些原本准备攻击他的批评者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 mollify</td><td>/ˈmɑːlɪfaɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>平息，抚慰，缓和</td><td>mollify an angry customer<br>安抚愤怒的顾客</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A sincere apology from the manager mollified the furious client.  经理诚挚的道歉安抚了这位愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 placate</td><td>/ˈpleɪkeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>（通过让步以）平息抚慰</td><td>placate the critics<br>平息批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The company offered full refunds to placate its angriest customers.  公司提供全额退款以平息最愤怒的客户。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 enrage</td><td>/enˈreɪdʒ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使愤怒，激怒</td><td>enrage the audience<br>激怒观众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His flippant apology only enraged the families further.  他轻率的道歉只让那些家庭更加愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 incense</td><td>/ɪnˈsens/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>incense the public<br>激怒公众</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The proposal is certain to incense feminists across the country.  这项提案肯定会激怒全国的女权主义者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inflame</td><td>/ɪnˈfleɪm/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使加剧</td><td>inflame public anger<br>使公众愤怒加剧</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's careless comments only inflamed public anger over the housing shortage.  市长轻率的言论只是加剧了公众对住房短缺的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 infuriate</td><td>/ɪnˈfjʊrieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒</td><td>infuriate the critics<br>激怒批评者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The decision to cut pensions infuriated thousands of workers across the country.  削减养老金的决定激怒了全国成千上万的工人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ire</td><td>/ˈaɪr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>愤怒，怒火</td><td>draw the ire of critics<br>引来批评者的怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new tax drew the ire of small business owners.  新税收招致了小企业主的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 madden</td><td>/ˈmædən/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使发狂，使愤怒</td><td>madden the audience<br>使观众暴怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The endless delays maddened passengers waiting at the gate.  无休止的延误让在登机口等待的乘客怒不可遏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 outrage</td><td>/ˈaʊˌtreɪdʒ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词、及物动词</td><td>愤怒；暴行；激怒</td><td>public outrage<br>公众的愤怒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The verdict caused outrage among the victim's family and friends.  判决引起受害者家人和朋友的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antagonize</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənaɪz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>与…敌对，反对；激怒</td><td>antagonize his allies<br>与他的盟友为敌</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was careful not to antagonize the allies he still needed.  他小心地避免与仍然需要的盟友为敌。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 arouse hostility</td><td>/əˈraʊz hɑˈstɪləti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>招致敌意</td><td>arouse hostility among neighbors<br>招致邻里敌意</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His blunt remarks aroused hostility in a room full of allies.  他直白的话在满是盟友的房间里招来了敌意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>propitious</b></td><td>/prəˈpɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吉祥的</td><td>a propitious moment<br>吉利的时机</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They waited for a propitious moment to announce the merger.  他们等待一个吉利的时机来宣布合并。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 auspicious</td><td>/ɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>好兆头的</td><td>an auspicious beginning<br>吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rain on the wedding day was considered an auspicious beginning.  婚礼当天下雨被认为是个好兆头的开端。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 encouraging</td><td>/ɪnˈkɜːrɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人鼓舞的</td><td>encouraging early results<br>令人鼓舞的初步结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The first trial produced encouraging results for the new treatment.  首轮试验为这种新疗法带来了令人鼓舞的结果。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fair</td><td>/ˈfer/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>公正的；相当的；晴朗的</td><td>a fair trial<br>公正的审判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone deserves a fair hearing before any decision is made.  在做出任何决定之前，人人都应得到公正的申辩机会。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 heartening</td><td>/ˈhɑːrtənɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人振奋的</td><td>a heartening response<br>令人振奋的反响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was heartening to see strangers helping clear the flooded street.  看到陌生人一起清理被淹的街道令人振奋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 optimistic</td><td>/ˌɑːptəˈmɪstɪk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观的</td><td>remain cautiously optimistic<br>保持谨慎乐观</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite the losses, the founders remained optimistic about next year.  尽管亏损，创始人们对明年依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promising</td><td>/ˈprɑːməsɪŋ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有前途的，有希望的</td><td>a promising young writer<br>有前途的青年作家</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is the most promising violinist the school has ever trained.  她是这所学校培养过的最有前途的小提琴手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 upbeat</td><td>/ˈʌpbiːt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐观向上的，令人振奋的</td><td>an upbeat mood<br>乐观的心情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Despite the heavy loss, the coach stayed upbeat about the coming season.  尽管惨败，教练对下个赛季依然乐观。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 dim</td><td>/ˈdɪm/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、动词</td><td>暗淡的，模糊的</td><td>a dim light<br>昏暗的灯光</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The room was too dim for reading anything but large print.  房间太暗，只能看大字。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 discouraging</td><td>/dɪsˈkɜːrɪdʒɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人沮丧的</td><td>discouraging news<br>令人沮丧的消息</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The discouraging test scores made him consider dropping the course.  令人沮丧的考试成绩让他考虑退掉这门课。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 disheartening</td><td>/dɪsˈhɑːrtənɪŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人泄气的</td><td>a disheartening defeat<br>令人泄气的失利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Losing by a single point was disheartening for the entire squad.  以一分之差落败让全队都很泄气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 futureless</del></td><td>/ˈfjutʃərləs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>没有前途的</td><td>a futureless job<br>没前途的工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He quit a futureless job and retrained as a nurse at thirty.  他辞掉没前途的工作，三十岁改学做护士。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hopeless</td><td>/ˈhoʊpləs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无望的，绝望的</td><td>a hopeless situation<br>毫无希望的局面</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The doctors described his condition as sadly hopeless.  医生们说他的病情令人遗憾地毫无希望。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inauspicious</td><td>/ˌɪnɔːˈspɪʃəs/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不吉利的，不祥的</td><td>an inauspicious start<br>不吉利的开端</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an inauspicious start by losing twice at home.  球队主场连输两场，开局不利。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfavorable</td><td>/ʌnˈfeɪvərəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不利的，不赞成的</td><td>unfavorable conditions<br>不利的条件</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Unfavorable weather delayed the launch by two full days.  不利的天气使发射延迟了整整两天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unpromising</td><td>/ʌnˈprɑːmɪsɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不被看好的，没希望的</td><td>an unpromising start<br>不被看好的开局</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team made an unpromising start but finished the season strongly.  球队开局不被看好，赛季末却表现强势。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 tious</del></td><td>/ʃəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>unpropitious 断行后的后半</td><td>tious ends unpropitious<br>断词后半接成原词</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Adding tious to unpropi- gives back the word unpropitious.  把 tious 接到 unpropi- 后面就还原成 unpropitious。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 9

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proponent</b></td><td>/prəˈpoʊnənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>建议者，支持者</td><td>a proponent of change<br>变革的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She became the leading proponent of change within the old institution.  她成了这家老机构内部倡导变革的主要支持者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 advocate</td><td>/ˈædvəkeɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持，提倡</td><td>advocate reform<br>提倡改革</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Many doctors advocate walking as the simplest form of exercise.  许多医生提倡把走路作为最简单的运动方式。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 apostle</td><td>/əˈpɑːsl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>（政策或思想等的）信奉者，支持者</td><td>an apostle of reform<br>改革的倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became a tireless apostle of reform in the state legislature.  他成了州议会中不懈的改革倡导者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 champion</td><td>/ˈtʃæmpiən/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>支持</td><td>champion a cause<br>支持一项事业</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has championed the rights of migrant workers for years.  她多年来一直为农民工的权益奔走呼吁。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 expounder</del></td><td>/ɪkˈspaʊndər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>阐述者，讲解者</td><td>an expounder of doctrine<br>教义的阐述者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He became the movement's clearest expounder and busiest public speaker.  他成了这场运动最清晰的阐述者和最忙的演讲人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 espouser</del></td><td>/ɪˈspaʊzər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，拥护者</td><td>an espouser of the cause<br>该事业的拥护者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 As an early espouser of the theory, he faced constant ridicule.  作为该理论的早期拥护者，他不断受到嘲笑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 friend</td><td>/ˈfrend/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>朋友，友人</td><td>a close friend<br>亲密的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She has been my closest friend since primary school.  她从小学起就是我最亲密的朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 promoter</td><td>/prəˈmoʊtər/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，倡导者；主办人</td><td>a promoter of literacy<br>识字运动的倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The concert promoter guaranteed the band a fee regardless of ticket sales.  演出主办方无论票房如何都保证付给乐队报酬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 supporter</td><td>/səˈpɔːrtər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>支持者，拥护者</td><td>a loyal supporter<br>忠诚的支持者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands of supporters gathered outside the stadium before kickoff.  开赛前数千名支持者聚集在体育场外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 protagonist</td><td>/proʊˈtæɡənəst/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>倡导者，支持者；主角</td><td>a leading protagonist<br>主要倡导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She was a leading protagonist of free public transport in the city.  她是本市免费公交的主要倡导者。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 adversary</td><td>/ˈædvərseri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>敌手，对手</td><td>a formidable adversary<br>强劲的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He respected his adversary even while fighting to win.  即使在拼命求胜时，他也尊重自己的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 antagonist</td><td>/ænˈtæɡənɪst/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，敌人</td><td>a bitter antagonist<br>势不两立的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her chief antagonist in the debate was a former professor.  她在辩论中的主要对手是一位前教授。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 opponent</td><td>/əˈpoʊnənt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>对手，反对者</td><td>a worthy opponent<br>值得尊敬的对手</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She defeated a much stronger opponent in the final round.  她在决赛中击败了一位强得多的对手。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 detractor</td><td>/dɪˈtræktər/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>批评者，贬损者</td><td>silence his detractors<br>让批评者闭嘴</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two winning seasons silenced most of the coach's detractors.  两个赛季的胜利让教练的多数批评者闭了嘴。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>propriety</b></td><td>/prəˈpraɪəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>适当，得体</td><td>a sense of propriety<br>得体的分寸感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her sense of propriety kept her from raising the subject at dinner.  她的得体分寸感使她没有在晚餐时提起这个话题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 appositeness</del></td><td>/ˈæpəzətnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贴切，恰当</td><td>the appositeness of the quote<br>引语的贴切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Everyone praised the appositeness of the poem she read aloud.  大家都赞叹她朗读那首诗的贴切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 aptness</del></td><td>/ˈæptnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>贴切，恰当；倾向</td><td>the aptness of the metaphor<br>比喻的贴切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The aptness of his metaphor made the whole argument suddenly clear.  他比喻的贴切让整个论点顿时清楚了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 felicity</td><td>/fɪˈlɪsəti/</td><td>★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>得体，恰当；幸福</td><td>felicity of expression<br>措辞得体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her essays are admired for their felicity of expression.  她的散文以措辞得体而受赞赏。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fitness</td><td>/ˈfɪtnəs/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>适当，恰当；健康</td><td>fitness for the post<br>胜任该职位</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee questioned his fitness for such a sensitive post.  委员会质疑他是否适合这样敏感的职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 properness</del></td><td>/ˈprɑpərnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>恰当，得体</td><td>the properness of his conduct<br>他举止的得体</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 No one could fault the properness of her behavior at dinner.  没人能挑出她晚宴上举止的不得体。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 rightness</td><td>/ˈraɪtnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>正确；恰当</td><td>the rightness of the decision<br>决定的正确</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years later he still defended the rightness of that difficult choice.  多年后他仍为那个艰难选择的正确性辩护。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 seemliness</del></td><td>/ˈsimlinəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>得体，合乎礼节</td><td>a sense of seemliness<br>得体的分寸感</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her grandmother valued seemliness above almost every other virtue.  她祖母把得体看得几乎重于一切美德。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 suitability</td><td>/ˌsuːtəˈbɪləti/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>适合，适宜</td><td>suitability for the role<br>是否适合该角色</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The panel debated her suitability for the demanding new role.  评审团讨论了她是否适合这个要求很高的新角色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 improperness</del></td><td>/ɪmˈprɑpərnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不当，失礼</td><td>the improperness of the joke<br>玩笑的失礼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Guests noticed the improperness of his toast at once.  客人们立刻察觉到他祝酒词的失礼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 impropriety</td><td>/ˌɪmprəˈpraɪəti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不得体，失礼；不当行为</td><td>financial impropriety<br>财务上的不当行为</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He resigned amid allegations of financial impropriety.  他在财务不当行为的指控中辞职。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inappositeness</del></td><td>/ɪˈnæpəzətnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不贴切，不恰当</td><td>the inappositeness of the analogy<br>类比的不贴切</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Critics pointed out the inappositeness of comparing the two crises.  批评者指出把两场危机相比并不贴切。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 inappropriateness</td><td>/ˌɪnəˈproʊpriətnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不恰当，不合适</td><td>the inappropriateness of the remark<br>言辞的不当</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He apologized for the inappropriateness of his remark about her family.  他为自己关于她家人的不当言辞道歉。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 inaptness</del></td><td>/ɪnˈæptnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不恰当；笨拙</td><td>the inaptness of the title<br>标题的不恰当</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Readers complained about the inaptness of the book's cheerful title.  读者抱怨这本书欢快的标题很不恰当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 infelicity</del></td><td>/ˌɪnfəˈlɪsəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>措辞不当；不幸</td><td>a stylistic infelicity<br>文风上的失当</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor marked every infelicity in the translated chapter.  编辑标出了译稿这一章里每处措辞失当。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfitness</td><td>/ʌnˈfɪtnəs/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不适合，不称职</td><td>unfitness for command<br>不适合担任指挥</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The inquiry cited his unfitness for command during the crisis.  调查指出他在危机中不适合担任指挥。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ness</td><td>/ˈnes/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>名词后缀，表性质或状态</td><td>the suffix ness<br>后缀 -ness</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Adding ness to unseemli- restores the noun unseemliness.  把 ness 接到 unseemli- 后面就还原成 unseemliness。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unsuitability</td><td>/ˌʌnsutəˈbɪləti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不适合，不相宜</td><td>unsuitability for the job<br>不适合这份工作</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His unsuitability for the job became obvious within a week.  他不适合这份工作，一周内就显而易见。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 wrongness</td><td>/ˈrɔŋnəs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>名词</td><td>错误；不当</td><td>the wrongness of the policy<br>政策的错谬</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She argued the wrongness of the policy in plain, patient language.  她用朴实耐心的语言论证这项政策的错谬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prosaic</b></td><td>/prəˈzeɪɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>单调的，常见的</td><td>prosaic details<br>平淡的细节</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Behind the legend lies a prosaic story about a lost receipt.  传说背后是一个关于遗失收据的平淡故事。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 commonplace</td><td>/ˈkɑːmənpleɪs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>平凡的；老生常谈</td><td>a commonplace observation<br>老生常谈的看法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Video calls have become commonplace in ordinary office life.  视频通话在日常办公中已很平常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 everyday</td><td>/ˈevrideɪ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>日常的，平常的</td><td>everyday household chores<br>日常家务</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The book finds poetry in everyday chores like washing dishes.  这本书在洗碗一类日常家务里找到诗意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 routine</td><td>/ruˈtiːn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>例行的；常规程序</td><td>a routine check<br>例行检查</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The problem was discovered during a routine inspection last week.  这个问题是上周例行检查时发现的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unexceptional</td><td>/ˌʌnɪkˈsɛpʃənəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平凡的，无特别之处的</td><td>an unexceptional performance<br>平淡无奇的表现</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The restaurant serves decent but unexceptional Italian food.  这家餐厅的意大利菜不错，但平淡无奇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 unremarkable</td><td>/ˌʌnrɪˈmɑːrkəbəl/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平凡的，不起眼的</td><td>an unremarkable town<br>毫不起眼的小镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He grew up in an unremarkable town two hours from the coast.  他在离海岸两小时车程的一个平凡小镇长大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 workaday</td><td>/ˈwɜːrkəˌdeɪ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>平凡的；日常的</td><td>workaday routine<br>平淡的日常</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her paintings turn workaday streets into something quietly strange.  她的画把平凡街道变得静静地陌生起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abnormal</td><td>/æbˈnɔːrməl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>异常的，不正常的</td><td>abnormal test results<br>异常的检测结果</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The scan showed nothing abnormal in her lungs.  扫描显示她的肺部没有异常。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exceptional</td><td>/ɪkˈsepʃənl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>例外的，特别的，非凡的</td><td>an exceptional talent<br>非凡的天赋</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She showed exceptional talent for mathematics at a very young age.  她在很小的年纪就表现出非凡的数学天赋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 extraordinary</td><td>/ɪkˈstrɔːrdəneri/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>非凡的，特别的</td><td>extraordinary courage<br>非凡的勇气</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It took extraordinary courage to report what she had seen.  举报她所见之事需要非凡的勇气。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 odd</td><td>/ˈɑːd/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；奇数的</td><td>an odd pair of socks<br>不成对的袜子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It seemed odd that nobody answered the door at noon.  中午没人应门，这显得有点奇怪。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 out-of-the-way</td><td>/ˌaʊt əv ðə ˈweɪ/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不寻常的，稀奇的；偏僻的</td><td>out-of-the-way facts<br>稀奇少见的知识</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She collects out-of-the-way facts that surprise even seasoned quiz players.  她收集稀奇少见的知识，连资深答题者都感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strange</td><td>/ˈstreɪndʒ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>奇怪的；陌生的</td><td>a strange noise upstairs<br>楼上奇怪的声响</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was a strange smell coming from the kitchen last night.  昨晚厨房里传出一股奇怪的味道。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 exciting</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪtɪŋ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>令人兴奋的，刺激的</td><td>an exciting flavor<br>令人兴奋的味道</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The chef added chili to give the sauce an exciting edge.  厨师加了辣椒，让酱汁有了刺激的味觉冲击。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 preternatural</td><td>/ˌpriːtərˈnætʃrəl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>超乎寻常的</td><td>preternatural calm<br>异乎寻常的平静</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pilot showed preternatural calm while the engine was clearly failing.  发动机明显故障时，飞行员表现出超乎寻常的镇定。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ingenious</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒiːniəs/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>聪明的，有创造才能的</td><td>an ingenious solution<br>巧妙的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She devised an ingenious method for storing rainwater during the dry season.  她想出了一个在旱季储存雨水的巧妙方法。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 imaginary</td><td>/ɪˈmædʒəneri/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>想象的，虚构的</td><td>an imaginary friend<br>想象出的朋友</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The boy blamed every mess on an imaginary friend.  男孩把每次弄乱都推给一个想象出的朋友。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proscribe</b></td><td>/proʊˈskraɪb/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，排斥</td><td>proscribe a political party<br>取缔某个政党</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The new regime moved quickly to proscribe every opposition political party.  新政权迅速采取行动取缔所有反对党。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ban</td><td>/ˈbæn/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>及物动词、名词</td><td>禁止；禁令</td><td>ban smoking indoors<br>禁止室内吸烟</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city voted to ban plastic bags in all supermarkets.  该市投票决定禁止所有超市使用塑料袋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enjoin</td><td>/ɪnˈdʒɔɪn/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>命令；（法律上）禁止</td><td>enjoin the strike<br>下令禁止罢工</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court enjoined the company from dumping waste in the river.  法院禁止该公司向河里倾倒废物。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interdict</td><td>/ˌɪntərˈdɪkt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止；拦截；封锁</td><td>interdict arms shipments<br>禁止武器运输</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Naval patrols were sent to interdict arms shipments along the coast.  海军巡逻队被派去拦截沿海的军火运输。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 outlaw</td><td>/ˈaʊtˌlɔː/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>宣布…非法，禁止</td><td>outlaw the practice<br>明令禁止这做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city outlawed single-use plastic bags three years ago.  三年前本市明令禁止一次性塑料袋。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prohibit</td><td>/proʊˈhɪbət/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>禁止，阻止</td><td>prohibit the sale of<br>禁止销售</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city prohibits overnight parking on this narrow street.  该市禁止在这条窄街上过夜停车。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 sanction</td><td>/ˈsæŋkʃn/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>批准，同意，认可</td><td>sanction the new policy<br>批准这项新政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The board refused to sanction the plan without a detailed budget.  董事会拒绝在没有详细预算的情况下批准该计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permit</td><td>/pərˈmɪt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>允许，准许；许可证</td><td>permit access<br>允许进入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The school does not permit phones during classroom hours.  学校不允许上课时间使用手机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 allow</td><td>/əˈlaʊ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>允许，准许</td><td>allow visitors inside<br>允许访客进入</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The library allows visitors to photograph the manuscripts without flash.  图书馆允许访客不开闪光灯拍摄手稿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 let</td><td>/ˈlet/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>让，允许</td><td>let him speak<br>让他发言</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Please let him finish his sentence before you object.  请让他说完再反驳。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 suffer</td><td>/ˈsʌfər/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>受苦，遭受；容忍</td><td>suffer from a headache<br>受头痛折磨</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands suffered from the drought that struck the region that summer.  那年夏天，数千人饱受该地区旱灾之苦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prosecution</b></td><td>/ˌprɑːsɪˈkjuːʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>实行，执行；起诉</td><td>the prosecution of the war<br>战争的推进</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The general devoted every resource to the prosecution of the long war.  将军把一切资源都投入到这场漫长战争的推进上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 accomplishment</td><td>/əˈkɑːmplɪʃmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>成就，成绩；技能</td><td>a remarkable accomplishment<br>非凡的成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Finishing the bridge in one year was a real accomplishment.  一年内建成这座桥是了不起的成就。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 achievement</td><td>/əˈtʃiːvmənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>成就，成绩</td><td>a lifetime achievement<br>终身成就</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her greatest achievement was building the school from nothing.  她最大的成就是从无到有建起这所学校。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 discharge</td><td>/dɪsˈtʃɑːrdʒ/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>释放；解雇</td><td>discharge the prisoner<br>释放囚犯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The court discharged the prisoner immediately after the new evidence appeared.  新证据出现后，法庭立即释放了那名囚犯。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enactment</td><td>/ɪˈnæktmənt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>制定，颁布（法律）</td><td>the enactment of the law<br>法律的颁布</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The enactment of the new law took almost four years.  这部新法的颁布用了将近四年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 execution</td><td>/ˌeksɪˈkjuːʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>执行，实施</td><td>flawless execution of the plan<br>计划的完美执行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The idea was clever, but its execution was clumsy and slow.  想法很妙，执行却笨拙缓慢。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fulfillment</td><td>/fʊlˈfɪlmənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>实现，履行；满足感</td><td>the fulfillment of a promise<br>承诺的履行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty marked the fulfillment of years of patient talks.  该条约标志着多年耐心谈判的实现。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 implementation</td><td>/ˌɪmpləmenˈteɪʃn/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>实施，执行</td><td>phased implementation<br>分阶段实施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Full implementation of the reform will begin next spring.  这项改革将于明年春天全面实施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 performance</td><td>/pərˈfɔːrməns/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词</td><td>履行，执行；表演</td><td>performance of a contract<br>合同的履行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Payment depends on the timely performance of every listed duty.  付款取决于所列各项义务的按时履行。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 perpetration</td><td>/ˌpərpəˈtreɪʃən/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>犯下（罪行），实施</td><td>the perpetration of fraud<br>实施欺诈</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Two clerks assisted in the perpetration of the payroll fraud.  两名职员协助实施了这起工资欺诈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pursuance</td><td>/pərˈsuəns/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>执行，追求</td><td>in pursuance of duty<br>履行职责之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The officer acted in pursuance of a clear written order.  这名军官是在执行一份明确书面命令。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonfulfillment</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnfʊlˈfɪlmənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>未履行，未实现</td><td>nonfulfillment of the contract<br>合同未获履行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The buyer sued over nonfulfillment of the delivery terms.  买方因交货条款未履行提起诉讼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 nonperformance</del></td><td>/ˌnɑnpərˈfɔrməns/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>不履行，未执行</td><td>nonperformance of duties<br>不履行职责</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Repeated nonperformance of basic duties cost him the position.  一再不履行基本职责让他失去了职位。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>proselytize</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːsələtaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>（使）改变信仰</td><td>proselytize new converts<br>劝人改变信仰</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missionaries tried to proselytize the villagers with very little success.  传教士试图使村民改变信仰，收效甚微。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 convert</td><td>/kənˈvɜːrt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>改变，转化</td><td>convert heat into energy<br>把热转化为能量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Engineers converted the abandoned warehouse into a bright public library.  工程师们把废弃的仓库改造成了明亮的公共图书馆。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prospect</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːspekt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>探查，勘探</td><td>prospect for gold<br>探寻金矿</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thousands came west to prospect for gold in the mountain streams.  成千上万的人西行到山间溪流中探寻金矿。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 hunt</td><td>/ˈhʌnt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>搜寻；打猎</td><td>hunt for clues<br>搜寻线索</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Divers hunted for wreckage along the seabed for three weeks.  潜水员在海床上搜寻残骸达三周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 probe</td><td>/proʊb/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>深入调查</td><td>probe deeply into the scandal<br>深入调查这桩丑闻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Investigators continued to probe deeply into the company's missing funds.  调查人员继续深入调查公司资金失踪的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 search</td><td>/ˈsɜːrtʃ/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>搜寻，搜查</td><td>search the whole area<br>搜遍整片区域</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Volunteers searched the woods until the light finally failed.  志愿者搜寻树林直到天色全暗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 skirr</del></td><td>/skɜːr/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>动词</td><td>疾走搜寻；疾飞作响</td><td>skirr across the moor<br>掠过荒原搜寻</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hounds skirred over the open field looking for a scent.  猎犬在空地上疾走搜寻气味。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prosperous</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːspərəs/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>成功的，繁盛的</td><td>a prosperous merchant town<br>繁盛的商镇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The harbor grew into a prosperous merchant town within a single generation.  这个港口在一代人的时间里发展成繁盛的商镇。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 booming</td><td>/ˈbuːmɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>迅速发展的，繁荣的</td><td>a booming housing market<br>火热的房地产市场</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A booming tech sector pushed rents beyond most salaries.  科技业的繁荣把租金推高到多数薪水之上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 flourishing</td><td>/ˈflɜːrɪʃɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁荣的，兴旺的</td><td>a flourishing trade<br>兴旺的贸易</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The port supported a flourishing trade in spices.  这座港口支撑起兴旺的香料贸易。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 halcyon</td><td>/ˈhælsiən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宁静的，平静的</td><td>halcyon days<br>宁静美好的日子</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He often talked about the halcyon days of his youth.  他常谈起年轻时那些宁静美好的日子。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lush</td><td>/lʌʃ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>茂盛的，繁茂的</td><td>lush vegetation<br>茂盛的植被</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Lush vegetation covered the valley floor after weeks of steady rain.  连续数周的降雨之后，茂盛的植被覆盖了谷底。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 palmy</td><td>/ˈpɑːmi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁荣的</td><td>the palmy days of radio<br>广播的繁荣年代</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He liked to reminisce about the palmy days of the family business.  他喜欢追忆家族生意繁荣的那些年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 roaring</td><td>/ˈrɔːrɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>咆哮的，轰鸣的</td><td>a roaring engine<br>轰鸣的引擎</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We could hardly talk over the roaring waterfall.  在轰鸣的瀑布声中我们几乎无法交谈。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thriving</td><td>/ˈθraɪvɪŋ/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>繁荣的，兴盛的</td><td>a thriving local economy<br>兴盛的地方经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The town has a thriving market every Saturday morning.  这个镇每周六上午都有兴旺的集市。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depressed</td><td>/dɪˈprest/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不开心的，情绪不高的，消沉的</td><td>feel deeply depressed<br>感到十分消沉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt depressed for weeks after the letter of rejection arrived.  收到拒信之后，她消沉了好几个星期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>prostrate</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːstreɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>平躺的，俯卧的；使极度虚弱</td><td>lie prostrate on the ground<br>平躺在地上</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The exhausted runner lay prostrate on the ground after crossing the line.  那名精疲力竭的跑者冲线后平躺在地上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitated</td><td>/dəˈbɪləˌteɪtɪd/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，衰弱的</td><td>debilitated by long illness<br>因久病而衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The patient looked pale and debilitated after months of treatment.  经过数月治疗，病人显得苍白虚弱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 effete</td><td>/ɪˈfiːt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，衰落的</td><td>an effete aristocracy<br>衰落的贵族阶层</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An effete aristocracy clung to its titles long after the wealth had vanished.  衰落的贵族阶层在财富早已消失后仍死守头衔。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enervated</td><td>/ˈɛnərveɪtəd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>虚弱的，无力的</td><td>enervated by the heat<br>被暑热弄得虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long illness left him enervated and unusually pale.  长期患病使他虚弱且异常苍白。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 enfeebled</td><td>/enˈfiːbəld/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>衰弱的，虚弱的</td><td>enfeebled by illness<br>因病而衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Enfeebled by fever, he could barely sit up.  因发烧而虚弱，他几乎坐不起来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 languid</td><td>/ˈlæŋɡwɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>没精打采的，虚弱的</td><td>a languid summer afternoon<br>慢悠悠的夏日午后</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A languid summer afternoon passed with nobody willing to leave the shade.  一个慢悠悠的夏日午后过去了，没人愿意离开树荫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sapped</td><td>/ˈsæpt/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>力气被耗尽的，虚弱的</td><td>sapped of energy<br>精力被耗尽</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The team looked sapped after three games in one week.  一周三场比赛后球队看来精力耗尽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 debilitate</td><td>/dɪˈbɪlɪteɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>debilitate the immune system<br>使免疫系统衰弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The virus gradually debilitates the immune system of healthy adults.  这种病毒会逐渐削弱健康成年人的免疫系统。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 devitalize</del></td><td>/diːˈvaɪtəlaɪz/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使失去活力</td><td>devitalize the local economy<br>使当地经济失去活力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of neglect devitalized the once thriving downtown district.  多年的忽视使曾经繁荣的市中心失去活力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 enervate</del></td><td>/ˈenərveɪt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱</td><td>enervate the whole team<br>使全队疲弱无力</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The long heat wave enervated the whole team before the final match.  漫长的热浪在决赛前使全队疲弱无力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
