<tr><td><del>↳近 enfeeble</del></td><td>/ɪnˈfiːbəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>使衰弱，使虚弱</td><td>enfeeble the patient<br>使病人虚弱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A long fever enfeebled him so much that walking hurt.  长期发烧使他虚弱到走路都疼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 late</td><td>/ˈleɪt/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、副词</td><td>晚的，迟的；已故的</td><td>late in the season<br>在季节的后期</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rain came too late to save the withered crops.  雨来得太晚，救不了枯萎的庄稼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sap</td><td>/sæp/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>削弱，逐渐耗尽</td><td>sap his strength<br>削弱他的力量</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Weeks of night shifts sapped the energy of the young doctor completely.  数周的夜班彻底削弱了这位年轻医生的精力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 tire</td><td>/ˈtaɪər/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>使疲劳，感到疲倦</td><td>tire easily<br>容易疲倦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Long meetings tire her far more than a hard morning run.  长会比清晨的剧烈跑步更让她疲倦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 erect</td><td>/ɪˈrekt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词、及物动词</td><td>直立的，垂直的</td><td>stand erect<br>笔直地站着</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old soldier still stood perfectly erect.  这位老兵仍站得笔直。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 upright</td><td>/ˈʌpraɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>正直的，诚实的；竖直的</td><td>an upright citizen<br>一位正直的公民</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Neighbors described him as an upright and reliable man.  邻居们说他是个正直可靠的人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 fortify</td><td>/ˈfɔːrtɪfaɪ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>加固；增强，强化</td><td>fortify a city wall<br>加固城墙</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The milk is fortified with extra vitamin D.  这种牛奶额外强化添加了维生素D。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 strengthen</td><td>/ˈstreŋθən/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>加强，增强</td><td>strengthen the economy<br>增强经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Regular exercise will strengthen your heart and lungs.  规律锻炼会增强你的心肺功能。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 beef up</td><td>/biːf ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>加强，充实</td><td>beef up security measures<br>加强安保措施</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city beefed up security before the international summit began.  国际峰会开始前，该市加强了安保。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>protean</b></td><td>/ˈproʊtiən/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>善变的；多才多艺的</td><td>a protean talent<br>多变的才华</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The actor's protean talent let him play villains and saints equally well.  这位演员多变的才华使他演反派和圣人都同样出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 adaptable</td><td>/əˈdæptəbəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>适应力强的；可改编的</td><td>a highly adaptable species<br>适应力极强的物种</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Crows are adaptable birds that thrive even in crowded cities.  乌鸦适应力很强，连拥挤的城市里也能繁盛。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 universal</td><td>/ˌjuːnəˈvɜːrsəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，全体的</td><td>universal human rights<br>普世人权</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Music seems to have a universal appeal across all cultures.  音乐似乎在所有文化中都有普遍的感染力。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 all-around</td><td>/ˌɔːl əˈraʊnd/</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>全面的，多才多艺的</td><td>an all-around athlete<br>全能运动员</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She is an all-around student who excels in sports and music.  她是个全面发展的学生，体育和音乐都很出色。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 static</td><td>/ˈstætɪk/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>静态的；停滞的</td><td>a static economy<br>停滞的经济</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wages have remained static while the cost of housing keeps rising.  房价不断上涨，工资却一直停滞不动。</td></tr>
</tbody>
</table>

## Unit 10

<table>
<thead><tr>
<th>单词</th>
<th>音标</th>
<th>常用</th>
<th>口语</th>
<th>词性</th>
<th>中文释义</th>
<th>常用短语</th>
</tr></thead>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>protocol</b></td><td>/ˈproʊtəkɔːl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词</td><td>正确的礼仪规范</td><td>diplomatic protocol<br>外交礼仪规范</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Diplomatic protocol requires that the older ambassador be greeted first.  外交礼仪规范要求先向资历较长的大使致意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>protract</b></del></td><td>/prəˈtrækt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖长</td><td>protract the negotiations<br>拖长谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Endless objections protracted the meeting into the evening.  无休止的反对把会议拖到了晚上。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 elongate</td><td>/ɪˈlɔːŋɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>拉长，延长</td><td>elongate the shape<br>拉长形状</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Tall mirrors elongate the room and make it look larger.  高镜子拉长了房间，使它显得更大。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lengthen</td><td>/ˈleŋθən/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>变长，加长</td><td>lengthen a skirt<br>把裙子放长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The days lengthen noticeably after the spring equinox.  春分之后白天明显变长。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prolong</td><td>/prəˈlɔːŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>延长，拖延</td><td>prolong someone's life<br>延长某人寿命</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 New treatments can prolong life by several years.  新疗法能把生命延长数年。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stretch</td><td>/ˈstretʃ/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>延长，拉长</td><td>stretch out the meeting<br>把会议拖长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They stretched the two-day trip into a leisurely full week.  他们把两天的行程拉长成悠闲的一整周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 drag out</td><td>/dræɡ ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>拖延，拉长</td><td>drag out the negotiations<br>拖延谈判</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides dragged out the talks for another eleven months.  双方又把谈判拖了十一个月。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 draw out</td><td>/drɔ ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>拖长，拉长；引出（话语）</td><td>draw out the vowels<br>把元音拖长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He drew out every word until the sentence seemed endless.  他把每个词都拖得很长，句子仿佛没完。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 curtail</td><td>/kɜːrˈteɪl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>缩短，削减</td><td>curtail public spending<br>削减公共开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Bad weather curtailed our trip by three days.  坏天气使我们的旅程缩短了三天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abridge</td><td>/əˈbrɪdʒ/</td><td>☆</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩短，删节</td><td>an abridged edition<br>删节版</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The publisher abridged the long novel for younger readers.  出版商为年轻读者删节了这部长篇小说。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 abbreviate</td><td>/əˈbriːvieɪt/</td><td>★</td><td>★★</td><td>动词</td><td>缩写，缩短</td><td>abbreviate a long title<br>缩写长标题</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 You may abbreviate 'Doctor' to 'Dr.' on the envelope.  信封上可以把 Doctor 缩写为 Dr.。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shorten</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词、不及物动词</td><td>缩短，删减</td><td>shorten the report<br>删短报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The editor shortened his rambling article by half.  编辑把他冗长的文章删短了一半。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cut back</td><td>/kʌt ˈbæk/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>削减，缩减</td><td>cut back on sugar<br>减少吃糖</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 We had to cut back on spending after the pay cut.  减薪后我们不得不削减开支。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>protrude</b></td><td>/proʊˈtruːd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突出</td><td>protrude from the wall<br>从墙上凸出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Rusty nails protrude from the wall, so please walk along the other side.  锈钉子从墙上凸出来，所以请沿另一侧走。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 bulge</td><td>/bʌldʒ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>凸起</td><td>a bulge in his pocket<br>他口袋里的鼓包</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A strange bulge in his jacket pocket caught the officer's attention.  他夹克口袋里奇怪的鼓包引起了警官的注意。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 overhang</td><td>/ˌoʊvərˈhæŋ/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>动词、名词</td><td>悬于…之上，突出</td><td>overhang the narrow street<br>悬在窄街上方</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Wooden balconies overhang the narrow street on both sides.  木质阳台从两侧悬在窄街上方。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 poke</td><td>/ˈpoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>缓慢行进，闲荡</td><td>poke along the road<br>沿路慢慢晃</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The old truck poked along the highway at thirty miles an hour.  那辆旧卡车以每小时三十英里在公路上慢慢晃。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 project</td><td>/prəˈdʒekt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★</td><td>不及物动词</td><td>突出，伸出</td><td>project from the wall<br>从墙面伸出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A stone ledge projects from the wall about two meters up.  一道石台从墙面约两米高处伸出。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stand out</td><td>/stænd ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>突出，显眼</td><td>stand out in a crowd<br>在人群中显眼</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her red coat made her stand out in the gray crowd.  她的红外套让她在灰色人群中格外显眼。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stick out</td><td>/stɪk ˈaʊt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>伸出，突出</td><td>stick out of the ground<br>从地面伸出</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A rusty pipe sticks out of the ground behind the shed.  一根锈管从棚屋后的地面伸出来。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concave</td><td>/kɑːnˈkeɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>凹的，凹面的</td><td>a concave mirror<br>凹面镜</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A concave lens spreads the light instead of focusing it.  凹透镜把光线发散而不是聚焦。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>protuberant</b></td><td>/proʊˈtuːbərənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>隆起的，凸出的</td><td>protuberant eyes<br>凸出的眼睛</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The frog's protuberant eyes let it watch the whole pond.  青蛙凸出的眼睛让它能看遍整个池塘。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 depressed</td><td>/dɪˈprest/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不开心的，情绪不高的，消沉的</td><td>feel deeply depressed<br>感到十分消沉</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She felt depressed for weeks after the letter of rejection arrived.  收到拒信之后，她消沉了好几个星期。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 concavity</td><td>/kɑːnˈkævəti/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>名词</td><td>凹陷，凹面</td><td>a slight concavity<br>略微的凹陷</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Water collects in a shallow concavity at the center of the stone.  水积在石头中央一处浅浅的凹陷里。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>provident</b></td><td>/ˈprɑːvɪdənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的；有远见的</td><td>a provident household<br>节俭有远见的家庭</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A provident household sets money aside long before hard times arrive.  节俭有远见的家庭远在困难到来之前就存下钱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 economical</td><td>/ˌekəˈnɑːmɪkəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节约的，经济的</td><td>an economical car<br>省油的汽车</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A small diesel engine is more economical on long journeys.  小型柴油发动机长途出行更省油。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sparing</td><td>/ˈsperɪŋ/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的，节约的</td><td>sparing use of salt<br>节省地用盐</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was sparing with salt because of his doctor's strict orders.  遵医嘱，他用盐十分节省。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 thrifty</td><td>/ˈθrɪfti/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>节俭的</td><td>a thrifty shopper<br>节俭的购物者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Thrifty habits helped her pay off the loan early.  节俭的习惯帮她提前还清了贷款。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 farsighted</td><td>/ˈfɑːrsaɪtəd/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；远视的</td><td>a farsighted policy<br>有远见的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The mayor's farsighted planning saved the city millions later.  市长有远见的规划后来为城市省下数百万。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 scrimpy</del></td><td>/ˈskrɪmpi/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>吝啬的；不足的</td><td>a scrimpy budget<br>拮据的预算</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They survived the winter on a scrimpy budget and a lot of soup.  他们靠拮据的预算和大量汤水过完了冬天。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 farseeing</del></td><td>/ˌfɑːrˈsiːɪŋ/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的</td><td>a farseeing leader<br>有远见的领导者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A farseeing mayor planted these trees fifty years ago.  一位有远见的市长五十年前种下了这些树。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 forehanded</del></td><td>/ˈfɔrˌhændɪd/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；节俭的</td><td>forehanded planning<br>有远见的筹划</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Forehanded farmers had already stored grain before the drought hit.  有远见的农民在旱灾来临前已存好粮食。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foreseeing</td><td>/fɔːrˈsiːɪŋ/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有先见之明的，预见的</td><td>a foreseeing planner<br>有远见的规划者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A foreseeing manager had stockpiled parts before the strike.  一位有先见之明的经理在罢工前囤了零件。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 forethoughtful</del></td><td>/ˈfɔːrθɔːtfəl/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>深谋远虑的</td><td>a forethoughtful decision<br>深谋远虑的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One forethoughtful engineer insisted on a backup generator.  一位深谋远虑的工程师坚持要装备用发电机。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 prescient</td><td>/ˈpreʃiənt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有先见之明的</td><td>a prescient warning<br>有先见之明的警告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His 1998 essay now looks remarkably prescient about the internet.  他一九九八年的文章如今看来对互联网极有先见之明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 proactive</td><td>/proʊˈæktɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>积极主动的，未雨绸缪的</td><td>a proactive approach<br>积极主动的做法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The clinic takes a proactive approach to seasonal flu each autumn.  诊所每年秋天都对季节性流感采取主动措施。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 visionary</td><td>/ˈvɪʒəneri/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>有远见的；不切实际的，空想的</td><td>a visionary founder<br>有远见的创始人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their visionary founder predicted the mobile market twenty years early.  他们有远见的创始人提前二十年预见了移动市场。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 profligate</td><td>/ˈprɑːflɪɡət/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>挥金如土的，挥霍的</td><td>profligate spending<br>挥霍无度的开支</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The council's profligate spending on ceremonies angered local taxpayers.  议会在典礼上挥霍无度的开支激怒了当地纳税人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 half-baked</td><td>/ˌhæfˈbeɪkt/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不成熟的，欠考虑的</td><td>a half-baked scheme<br>不成熟的方案</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They rushed ahead with a half-baked plan.  他们仓促推行了一个不成熟的计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 half-cocked</td><td>/ˈhæfˌkɑːkt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>准备不足的，草率的</td><td>go off half-cocked<br>仓促行事</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They went off half-cocked and launched the product without testing.  他们仓促行事，产品未测试就上线了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳反 improvident</del></td><td>/ɪmˈprɑvɪdənt/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>不节俭的；无远见的</td><td>improvident spending habits<br>不节俭的花钱习惯</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years of improvident borrowing left the family with nothing.  多年无节制的借贷让这家人一无所有。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 myopic</td><td>/maɪˈɑːpɪk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>缺乏远见的，缺乏辨别能力的</td><td>a myopic policy<br>缺乏远见的政策</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The city's myopic policy on housing ignored problems that residents saw daily.  这座城市在住房上缺乏远见的政策忽视了居民每天都看得见的问题。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 shortsighted</td><td>/ˈʃɔːrtsaɪtɪd/</td><td>★☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短视的，缺乏远见的；近视的</td><td>a shortsighted decision<br>短视的决定</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Cutting research funding now would be extremely shortsighted.  现在削减研究经费是极其短视的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>providential</b></td><td>/ˌprɑːvɪˈdenʃl/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>天意般的，幸运的</td><td>a providential rescue<br>天意般的获救</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their providential rescue came just minutes before the boat sank.  他们天意般的获救就发生在船沉前几分钟。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 lucky</td><td>/ˈlʌki/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>幸运的</td><td>a lucky escape<br>幸运的脱险</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They had a lucky escape when the roof collapsed.  屋顶塌落时他们幸运地脱险了。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fluky</td><td>/ˈfluːki/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>侥幸的</td><td>a fluky victory<br>侥幸的胜利</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A fluky coincidence put me indoors when the blizzard finally struck.  一个侥幸的巧合让暴风雪来袭时我正待在室内。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fortuitous</td><td>/fɔːrˈtuːɪtəs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>偶然的，碰巧的</td><td>a fortuitous meeting<br>一次偶然的相遇</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Their fortuitous meeting at the airport changed both careers.  他们在机场的偶然相遇改变了两人的职业道路。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unfortunate</td><td>/ʌnˈfɔːrtʃənət/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的，遗憾的</td><td>an unfortunate mistake<br>不幸的失误</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 It was unfortunate that the letter arrived a week too late.  遗憾的是那封信迟到了一周。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 mishap</td><td>/ˈmɪshæp/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>名词</td><td>小事故；不幸之事</td><td>a minor mishap<br>小事故</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A minor mishap in the kitchen delayed dinner by nearly an hour.  厨房里的一个小事故让晚饭推迟了将近一小时。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 hapless</td><td>/ˈhæpləs/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>倒霉的，不幸的</td><td>the hapless victim<br>倒霉的受害者</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The hapless traveler lost both his passport and wallet.  这位倒霉的旅客丢了护照和钱包。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-fated</td><td>/ˌɪl ˈfeɪtɪd/</td><td>★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>注定不幸的，命运多灾的</td><td>an ill-fated expedition<br>注定不幸的远征</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Only two men returned from that ill-fated expedition.  那次注定不幸的远征只有两人生还。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ill-starred</td><td>/ˌɪlˈstɑːrd/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的，倒霉的</td><td>an ill-starred voyage<br>不幸的航行</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The ill-starred expedition lost two ships in the first month.  这次不幸的远征第一个月就损失了两艘船。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 luckless</td><td>/ˈlʌkləs/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>不幸的，倒霉的</td><td>a luckless gambler<br>倒霉的赌徒</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The luckless team lost every match of the season.  这支倒霉的球队本赛季场场皆输。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 star-crossed</td><td>/ˈstɑr ˌkrɔst/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>命运不济的</td><td>star-crossed lovers<br>命运多舛的恋人</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The play follows two star-crossed lovers from rival families.  这部戏讲述两个来自仇家、命运多舛的恋人。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>provincial</b></td><td>/prəˈvɪnʃl/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>狭隘（的）</td><td>provincial tastes<br>狭隘的品味</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His provincial tastes made him suspicious of anything foreign or unfamiliar.  他狭隘的品味使他对一切外来或陌生的事物都怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 illiberal</td><td>/ɪˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>心胸狭窄的；不开明的</td><td>illiberal attitudes<br>不开明的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His illiberal views on the arts surprised his younger colleagues.  他对艺术的狭隘看法让年轻同事很惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 insular</td><td>/ˈɪnsələr/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>（观念、想法等）孤立狭隘的</td><td>an insular attitude<br>狭隘的态度</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His insular attitude toward foreign customs cost him many friendships abroad.  他对外国习俗的狭隘态度使他在国外失去了许多友谊。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 parochial</td><td>/pəˈroʊkiəl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>a parochial outlook<br>狭隘的眼界</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His parochial outlook kept him from appreciating other cultures.  他狭隘的眼界使他无法欣赏其他文化。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 sectarian</td><td>/sekˈteriən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>狭隘的</td><td>sectarian prejudice<br>狭隘的派系偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His sectarian views blinded him to the merits of other traditions.  他狭隘的派系观念使他看不到其他传统的优点。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 narrow-minded</td><td>/ˌnæroʊ ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>心胸狭窄的，偏狭的</td><td>narrow-minded prejudice<br>狭隘的偏见</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His narrow-minded remarks embarrassed everyone at the table.  他狭隘的言论让全桌人尴尬。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 ecumenical</td><td>/ˌiːkjuːˈmenɪkl/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>世界范围的，普遍性的</td><td>an ecumenical council<br>世界性的宗教会议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 An ecumenical council brought together leaders from every branch of the church.  一次世界性的宗教会议把教会各支派的领袖聚在一起。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 broad-minded</td><td>/ˌbrɔd ˈmaɪndəd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的，宽容的</td><td>a broad-minded editor<br>开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her broad-minded parents let her study art abroad.  她开明的父母让她出国学艺术。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 catholic</td><td>/ˈkæθlɪk/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>形容词</td><td>普遍的，包容的</td><td>catholic tastes<br>广泛的爱好</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Her catholic tastes in music surprised everyone at the party.  她在音乐上广泛的爱好让聚会上每个人都惊讶。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 cosmopolitan</td><td>/ˌkɑːzməˈpɑːlɪtən/</td><td>★★</td><td>★</td><td>形容词、名词</td><td>有世界性眼光的，包容的</td><td>a cosmopolitan city<br>国际化的都市</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Years abroad gave her a cosmopolitan outlook her old classmates clearly lacked.  多年国外生活给了她老同学明显缺乏的世界性眼光。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 liberal</td><td>/ˈlɪbərəl/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想前卫的；慷慨的，大方的</td><td>liberal social views<br>前卫的社会观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His liberal social views often surprised the older members of the club.  他前卫的社会观念常常让俱乐部里年长的成员感到意外。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open</td><td>/ˈoʊpən/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>公开的，坦率的</td><td>open opposition<br>公开的反对</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 There was open opposition to the plan at the meeting.  会上有人公开反对这项计划。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 open-minded</td><td>/ˈoʊpən ˈmaɪndɪd/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>思想开明的</td><td>an open-minded editor<br>思想开明的编辑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good reviewer stays open-minded about unfamiliar musical styles.  好的评论者对陌生的音乐风格保持开明。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 receptive</td><td>/rɪˈseptɪv/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>形容词</td><td>乐于接受的，开明的</td><td>receptive to new ideas<br>乐于接受新观念</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee proved unexpectedly receptive to her radical proposal.  委员会出乎意料地乐于接受她激进的提案。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 tolerant</td><td>/ˈtɑːlərənt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>宽容的，容忍的</td><td>tolerant of criticism<br>能容忍批评</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 A good editor is tolerant of odd but honest writing.  好编辑对古怪却真诚的文字是宽容的。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>provisional</b></td><td>/prəˈvɪʒənl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>临时的</td><td>a provisional agreement<br>临时协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The two sides signed a provisional agreement pending final legal review.  双方签署了一份临时协议，等待最终的法律审查。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 impermanent</td><td>/ɪmˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>非永久的，短暂的</td><td>impermanent beauty<br>短暂的美</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 All fashions are impermanent, replaced by new styles within a few seasons.  一切时尚都是短暂的，几季之内就被新风格取代。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interim</td><td>/ˈɪntərɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>中间过渡时期，间隔；暂时的</td><td>an interim report<br>中期报告</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The committee published an interim report while the investigation was still continuing.  委员会在调查仍在进行时发布了一份中期报告。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del>↳近 provisionary</del></td><td>/prəˈvɪʒəˌnɛri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>临时的，暂定的</td><td>a provisionary agreement<br>临时协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides signed a provisionary agreement pending final review.  双方签了一份临时协议，等待最终审核。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 short-term</td><td>/ˌʃɔːrtˈtɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>短期的</td><td>a short-term contract<br>短期合同</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 She took a short-term contract to cover someone's parental leave.  她接了份短期合同，代人休育儿假的岗。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 long-term</td><td>/ˌlɔːŋˈtɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>长期的</td><td>long-term investment<br>长期投资</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Forests are a long-term investment that nobody sees repaid quickly.  森林是一项长期投资，没人能很快看到回报。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permanent</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>a permanent solution<br>永久的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They are seeking a permanent solution rather than another quick fix.  他们在寻找永久的解决办法，而不是又一个权宜之计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><del><b>provisory</b></del></td><td>/prəˈvaɪzəri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>有附带条件的；临时的</td><td>a provisory clause<br>附带条件的条款</td></tr>

</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 interim</td><td>/ˈɪntərɪm/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>名词、形容词</td><td>中间过渡时期，间隔；暂时的</td><td>an interim report<br>中期报告</td></tr>

<tr><td>↳近 provisional</td><td>/prəˈvɪʒənl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>临时的</td><td>a provisional agreement<br>临时协议</td></tr>

<tr><td><del>↳近 provisionary</del></td><td>/prəˈvɪʒəˌnɛri/</td><td>☆</td><td>☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>临时的，暂定的</td><td>a provisionary agreement<br>临时协议</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Both sides signed a provisionary agreement pending final review.  双方签了一份临时协议，等待最终审核。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 unconditional</td><td>/ˌʌnkənˈdɪʃənəl/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>无条件的，绝对的</td><td>unconditional surrender<br>无条件投降</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The treaty demanded the unconditional withdrawal of all foreign troops.  条约要求所有外国军队无条件撤离。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 long-term</td><td>/ˌlɔːŋˈtɜːrm/</td><td>★★★★</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>长期的</td><td>long-term investment<br>长期投资</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Forests are a long-term investment that nobody sees repaid quickly.  森林是一项长期投资，没人能很快看到回报。</td></tr>

<tbody>
<tr><td>↳反 permanent</td><td>/ˈpɜːrmənənt/</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>★★★☆</td><td>形容词</td><td>永恒的</td><td>a permanent solution<br>永久的解决办法</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 They are seeking a permanent solution rather than another quick fix.  他们在寻找永久的解决办法，而不是又一个权宜之计。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td><b>provoke</b></td><td>/prəˈvoʊk/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激怒；驱使，激起</td><td>provoke an angry response<br>引发愤怒的回应</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His careless remark provoked an angry response from the whole audience.  他不慎的言论激起了全体观众的愤怒回应。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 arouse</td><td>/əˈraʊz/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>及物动词</td><td>唤起，激起；唤醒</td><td>arouse public suspicion<br>引起公众怀疑</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The missing files aroused suspicion among the auditors.  丢失的文件引起了审计人员的怀疑。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 excite</td><td>/ɪkˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，引发；使兴奋</td><td>excite public interest<br>激起公众兴趣</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 His speech excited strong resentment among the workers.  他的演讲激起了工人们的强烈不满。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 incite</td><td>/ɪnˈsaɪt/</td><td>★★</td><td>★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>incite a riot<br>煽动暴乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with inciting a riot outside the county courthouse.  他被指控在县法院外煽动暴乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 instigate</td><td>/ˈɪnstɪɡeɪt/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>煽动，激起</td><td>instigate a rebellion<br>煽动叛乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Several officers were accused of helping to instigate the rebellion in the province.  几名军官被指控参与煽动该省的叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 pique</td><td>/piːk/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起（兴趣）；使生气</td><td>pique her curiosity<br>激起她的好奇心</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The strange title piqued her curiosity, so she borrowed the book.  奇怪的书名激起了她的好奇心，于是她借走了那本书。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stimulate</td><td>/ˈstɪmjəˌleɪt/</td><td>★★☆</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>刺激，激发；促进</td><td>stimulate economic growth<br>促进经济增长</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Warm rain stimulated the seeds to sprout within a week.  温暖的雨水刺激种子一周内发芽。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir</td><td>/ˈstɜːr/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>激起，煽动；搅拌</td><td>stir up anger<br>激起怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The article stirred up anger among local residents.  这篇文章激起了当地居民的愤怒。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 fire up</td><td>/ˈfaɪər ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★★☆</td><td>★★★★</td><td>动词</td><td>激起，点燃（热情）</td><td>fire up the crowd<br>点燃观众热情</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 One short speech fired up the whole exhausted team.  一段简短讲话点燃了整支疲惫队伍的斗志。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 abet</td><td>/əˈbet/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>动词</td><td>帮助，教唆（做坏事）</td><td>aid and abet<br>帮助并教唆</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 He was charged with aiding and abetting the robbery.  他被控协助并教唆抢劫。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 ferment</td><td>/ˈfɜːrment/</td><td>★☆</td><td>★☆</td><td>名词、动词</td><td>动乱，骚动；发酵</td><td>in a state of ferment<br>处于动荡之中</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The whole country was in a state of ferment before the election.  大选前全国处于动荡之中。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 foment</td><td>/foʊˈment/</td><td>★</td><td>★</td><td>及物动词</td><td>助长，煽动</td><td>foment unrest<br>煽动动乱</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The pamphlets were designed to foment rebellion in the provinces.  这些小册子意在煽动各省叛乱。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 stir up</td><td>/stɜːr ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>激起，煽动</td><td>stir up trouble<br>挑起麻烦</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 The rumor stirred up more anger than the original decision.  这条谣言激起的怒火比原来的决定还多。</td></tr>
</tbody>
<tbody>
<tr><td>↳近 whip up</td><td>/wɪp ˈʌp/</td><td>★★★</td><td>★★☆</td><td>动词</td><td>煽起，激起；迅速做出</td><td>whip up public anger<br>煽起公众怒火</td></tr>
<tr><td colspan="7">例 Talk radio whipped up public anger against the new tax.  电台谈话节目煽起了公众对新税的怒火。</td></tr>
</tbody>
